104	    // 내부 광원 — 중앙 살짝 아래 오프셋 (속에서 빛나는 느낌)
105	    gfx.fillStyle(0xaa1100, 0.95); gfx.fillCircle(1, 2, 8);
106	    gfx.fillStyle(0xdd2200, 0.80); gfx.fillCircle(1, 2, 5);
107	    gfx.fillStyle(0xff5500, 0.55); gfx.fillCircle(1, 3, 2.5);
108	    // 반사 하이라이트 — 왼쪽 위 (구슬 3D 효과)
109	    gfx.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.90); gfx.fillCircle(-3.5, -3.5, 2.5);
110	    gfx.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.40); gfx.fillCircle(-2.5, -5, 1);
111	    gfx.setPosition(x, -20);
112	
113	    scene.tweens.add({
114	      targets: gfx, alpha: { from: 0.72, to: 1 },
115	      yoyo: true, repeat: -1, duration: 720, ease: 'Sine.easeInOut',
116	    });
117	
118	    this.yeoijus.push({ gfx, x, y: -20 });
119	  }
120	
121	  private _updateYeoijus(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
122	    const delta = api.scene.game.loop.delta * 0.001;
123	    const { player } = api;
124	    const fallDelta = (api.baseSpeed + api.difficultyLevel * 40) * delta;
125	    const r2 = MUGI_PARAMS.yeoijuCollectRadius ** 2;
126	    const remaining: YeoijuOrb[] = [];
127	
128	    for (const orb of this.yeoijus) {
129	      orb.y += fallDelta;
130	      orb.gfx.setPosition(orb.x, orb.y);
131	
132	      const dx = player.x - orb.x;
133	      const dy = player.y - orb.y;
134	      if (dx * dx + dy * dy < r2) {
135	        this._collectYeoiju(orb, api);
136	        continue;
137	      }
138	      if (orb.y > 660) { orb.gfx.destroy(); continue; }
139	      remaining.push(orb);
140	    }
141	    this.yeoijus = remaining;
142	  }
143	
144	  private _collectYeoiju(orb: YeoijuOrb, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
145	    this._spawnCollectEffect(orb.x, orb.y, api.scene);
146	    orb.gfx.destroy();
147	    api.addAbilityBonus(80);
148	
149	    // Revival recharge: runs regardless of gold form
150	    if (this.revivalCharges === 0 && this.totalRevivalsUsed < 3) {
151	      this.revivalChargeCount++;
152	      if (this.revivalChargeCount >= MUGI_PARAMS.goldThreshold) {
153	        this.revivalCharges = 1;
154	        this.revivalChargeCount = 0;
155	        this._playRechargeGlow(api.player.x, api.player.y, api.scene);
156	      }
157	      this._updateRevivalUI();
158	    }
159	
160	    if (this.isGoldForm) {
161	      this._strikeThunder(api, false);
162	      return;
163	    }
164	
165	    this.yeoijuCount++;
166	    this._updateCounter();
167	
168	    if (this.yeoijuCount >= MUGI_PARAMS.goldThreshold) {
169	      this._transformGold(api);
170	    } else {
171	      this._strikeRedLightning(api);
172	    }
173	  }
174	
175	  // ── 기운 (아우라) 이펙트 ─────────────────────────────────────────────
176	
177	  private _tickAura(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
178	    const level = this.isGoldForm ? 4 : Math.floor(this.yeoijuCount / 10);
179	    const delta = api.scene.game.loop.delta;
180	
181	    // 레벨별 검은/금색 기운
182	    if (level > 0) {
183	      this.auraTimer += delta;
184	      const interval = Math.max(70, 400 - level * 70);
185	      if (this.auraTimer >= interval) {
186	        this.auraTimer -= interval;
187	        const color = this.isGoldForm ? 0xffdd00 : 0x8800ff;
188	        for (let i = 0; i < level; i++) {
189	          this._spawnAuraWisp(api.player.x, api.player.y, color, level, api.scene);
190	        }
191	      }
192	    }
193	
194	    // 부활 충전 시 흰색 기운 (레벨 무관, 항상 일정 속도)
195	    if (this.revivalCharges > 0 && this.totalRevivalsUsed < 3) {
196	      this.revivalAuraTimer += delta;
197	      if (this.revivalAuraTimer >= 180) {
198	        this.revivalAuraTimer -= 180;
199	        this._spawnAuraWisp(api.player.x, api.player.y, 0xffffff, 3, api.scene);
200	        this._spawnAuraWisp(api.player.x, api.player.y, 0xffffff, 3, api.scene);
201	      }
202	    }
203	  }
204	
205	  // glow_dot + ADD 블렌드로 여우불 질감 표현
206	  private _spawnAuraWisp(px: number, py: number, color: number, level: number, scene: Phaser.Scene): void {
207	    const angle  = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
208	    const spread = 18 + level * 4;
209	    const ox = px + Math.cos(angle) * Phaser.Math.Between(8, spread);
210	    const oy = py + Math.sin(angle) * Phaser.Math.Between(8, spread * 0.6);
211	    const sz  = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(3, 5 + level * 0.6);
212	    const glowScale = sz / 28;
213	
214	    // 외곽 글로우 (ADD 블렌드 — 뒤 화면색에 색을 더함)
215	    const img = scene.add.image(ox, oy, 'glow_dot')
216	      .setDepth(6)
217	      .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD)
218	      .setTint(color)
219	      .setScale(glowScale);
220	
221	    // 코어: 외곽 글로우색 → 중심 흰색으로 그라데이션 느낌
222	    const core = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(7).setPosition(ox, oy);
223	    core.fillStyle(color, 0.85);
224	    core.fillCircle(0, 0, Math.max(2, sz * 0.45));
225	    core.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.9);
226	    core.fillCircle(0, 0, Math.max(1, sz * 0.22));
227	
228	    const tx  = ox + Phaser.Math.Between(-14, 14);
229	    const ty  = oy - Phaser.Math.Between(22, 44);
230	    const dur = Phaser.Math.Between(480, 860);
231	
232	    scene.tweens.add({
233	      targets: img, x: tx, y: ty, alpha: 0, scale: glowScale * 0.3,
234	      duration: dur, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
235	      onComplete: () => { if (img.active) img.destroy(); },
236	    });
237	    scene.tweens.add({
238	      targets: core, x: tx, y: ty, alpha: 0, scale: 0.3,
239	      duration: dur, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
240	      onComplete: () => { if (core.active) core.destroy(); },
241	    });
242	  }
243	
244	  private _spawnCollectEffect(x: number, y: number, scene: Phaser.Scene): void {
245	    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
246	      const angle = (i / 6) * Math.PI * 2;
247	      const g = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(160);
248	      g.fillStyle(0xff3333, 1);
249	      g.fillCircle(0, 0, Phaser.Math.Between(2, 4));
250	      g.setPosition(x, y);
251	      scene.tweens.add({
252	        targets: g,
253	        x: x + Math.cos(angle) * Phaser.Math.Between(12, 28),
254	        y: y + Math.sin(angle) * Phaser.Math.Between(12, 28),
255	        alpha: 0, scale: 0,
256	        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(200, 360),
257	        ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
258	        onComplete: () => g.destroy(),
259	      });
260	    }
261	  }
262	
263	  private _updateCounter(): void {
264	    if (!this.counterText) return;
265	    this.counterText.setText(`여의주 ${this.yeoijuCount}/${MUGI_PARAMS.goldThreshold}`);
266	  }
267	
268	  private _updateRevivalUI(): void {
269	    if (!this.revivalText) return;
270	    if (this.totalRevivalsUsed >= 3) {
271	      this.revivalText.setText('(부활 소진)').setColor('#666666');
272	    } else if (this.revivalCharges > 0) {
273	      this.revivalText.setText(`♡ 부활 (${3 - this.totalRevivalsUsed}회 남음)`).setColor('#ff66bb');
274	    } else {
275	      this.revivalText
276	        .setText(`○ 부활 충전 ${this.revivalChargeCount}/${MUGI_PARAMS.goldThreshold}`)
277	        .setColor('#aaaaaa');
278	    }
279	  }
280	
281	  // ── 황금 변신 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
282	
283	  private _transformGold(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
284	    this.isGoldForm = true;
285	    this.yeoijus.forEach(orb => orb.gfx.destroy());
286	    this.yeoijus = [];
287	    this.counterText?.setVisible(false);
288	    this._strikeThunder(api, true);
289	  }
290	
291	  private _resetGoldForm(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
292	    this.isGoldForm = false;
293	    this.yeoijuCount = 0;
294	    api.player.setTexturePrefix(this.normalPrefix);
295	    this.counterText?.setVisible(true);
296	    this._updateCounter();
297	  }
298	
299	  // ── 황금 번개 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
300	
301	  // applyGoldSprite: true → 변신 시 (180ms 딜레이 후 gold 텍스처 적용), false → 이미 변신 상태
302	  private _strikeThunder(api: GameSceneAPI, applyGoldSprite: boolean): void {
303	    const { scene, player } = api;
304	    const tx = player.x;
305	    const ty = player.y;
306	
307	    scene.cameras.main.flash(600, 255, 230, 0, true);
308	
309	    const g = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(310);
310	
311	    const makePts = (startX: number) => {
312	      const pts: { x: number; y: number }[] = [{ x: startX, y: -10 }];
313	      for (let i = 1; i < 17; i++) {
314	        const t = i / 17;
315	        const spread = 70 * (1 - t * 0.65);
316	        pts.push({ x: tx + Phaser.Math.Between(-spread, spread), y: (ty + 20) * t - 10 });
317	      }
318	      pts.push({ x: tx, y: ty + 8 });
319	      return pts;
320	    };
321	
322	    const drawBolt = () => {
323	      g.clear();
324	      const pts = makePts(tx + Phaser.Math.Between(-25, 25));
325	      g.lineStyle(44, 0xffaa00, 0.09); this._drawPolyline(g, pts);
326	      g.lineStyle(22, 0xffcc00, 0.32); this._drawPolyline(g, pts);
327	      g.lineStyle(9,  0xffee44, 0.80); this._drawPolyline(g, pts);
328	      g.lineStyle(3,  0xffffff, 1.00); this._drawPolyline(g, pts);
329	      [3, 7, 11].forEach(bi => {
330	        if (bi >= pts.length - 1) return;
331	        const bp = pts[bi]!;
332	        const dir = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1;
333	        const len = Phaser.Math.Between(55, 105);
334	        const bPts = [
335	          { x: bp.x, y: bp.y },
336	          { x: bp.x + dir * len * 0.35 + Phaser.Math.Between(-18, 18), y: bp.y + len * 0.33 },
337	          { x: bp.x + dir * len * 0.68 + Phaser.Math.Between(-12, 12), y: bp.y + len * 0.65 },
338	          { x: bp.x + dir * len + Phaser.Math.Between(-8, 8),          y: bp.y + len },
339	        ];
340	        g.lineStyle(9,   0xffcc00, 0.40); this._drawPolyline(g, bPts);
341	        g.lineStyle(3,   0xffee44, 0.75); this._drawPolyline(g, bPts);
342	        g.lineStyle(1.5, 0xffffff, 0.90); this._drawPolyline(g, bPts);
343	      });
344	    };
345	
346	    drawBolt();
347	    [50, 100, 150, 200].forEach(delay => {
348	      scene.time.delayedCall(delay, () => {
349	        if (!scene.sys.isActive() || !g.active) return;
350	        drawBolt();
351	      });
352	    });
353	
354	    scene.tweens.add({
355	      targets: g, alpha: 0, delay: 240, duration: 380, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
356	      onComplete: () => g.destroy(),
357	    });
358	
359	    const impact = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(308);
360	    impact.fillStyle(0xffee00, 0.7);
361	    impact.fillCircle(tx, ty, 55);
362	    scene.tweens.add({
363	      targets: impact, scaleX: 2.8, scaleY: 2.8, alpha: 0,
364	      duration: 500, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
365	      onComplete: () => impact.destroy(),
366	    });
367	
368	    const ring = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(309);
369	    ring.lineStyle(4, 0xffffff, 1);
370	    ring.strokeCircle(tx, ty, 40);
371	    scene.tweens.add({
372	      targets: ring, scaleX: 3.5, scaleY: 3.5, alpha: 0,
373	      duration: 450, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
374	      onComplete: () => ring.destroy(),
375	    });
376	
377	    scene.time.delayedCall(180, () => {
378	      if (!scene.sys.isActive()) return;
379	      // 변신 도중 부활로 isGoldForm이 해제됐을 수 있으므로 확인
380	      if (applyGoldSprite && this.isGoldForm) {
381	        api.player.setTexturePrefix('gold_mugi_');
382	      }
383	      this._clearPoops(api, () => true, 20);
384	    });
385	  }
386	
387	  // ── 검붉은 번개 (변신 전) ────────────────────────────────────────────
388	
389	  private _strikeRedLightning(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
390	    const { scene, player } = api;
391	    const tx = player.x;
392	    const ty = player.y;
393	
394	    scene.cameras.main.flash(250, 120, 0, 0, true);
395	
396	    const g = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(310);
397	
398	    // _strikeThunder와 동일한 형태, 색만 검붉은 팔레트
399	    const makePts = (startX: number) => {
400	      const pts: { x: number; y: number }[] = [{ x: startX, y: -10 }];
401	      for (let i = 1; i < 17; i++) {
402	        const t = i / 17;
403	        const spread = 70 * (1 - t * 0.65);
404	        pts.push({ x: tx + Phaser.Math.Between(-spread, spread), y: (ty + 20) * t - 10 });
405	      }
406	      pts.push({ x: tx, y: ty + 8 });
407	      return pts;
408	    };
409	
410	    const drawRed = () => {
411	      g.clear();
412	      const pts = makePts(tx + Phaser.Math.Between(-25, 25));
413	      g.lineStyle(30, 0x440000, 0.09); this._drawPolyline(g, pts);
414	      g.lineStyle(14, 0x880000, 0.32); this._drawPolyline(g, pts);
415	      g.lineStyle(6,  0x080000, 0.92); this._drawPolyline(g, pts); // 코어 바로 외곽 — 진한 검정
416	      g.lineStyle(2,  0xff3311, 1.00); this._drawPolyline(g, pts);
417	      [3, 7, 11].forEach(bi => {
418	        if (bi >= pts.length - 1) return;
419	        const bp = pts[bi]!;
420	        const dir = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1;
421	        const len = Phaser.Math.Between(55, 105);
422	        const bPts = [
423	          { x: bp.x, y: bp.y },
424	          { x: bp.x + dir * len * 0.35 + Phaser.Math.Between(-18, 18), y: bp.y + len * 0.33 },
425	          { x: bp.x + dir * len * 0.68 + Phaser.Math.Between(-12, 12), y: bp.y + len * 0.65 },
426	          { x: bp.x + dir * len + Phaser.Math.Between(-8, 8),          y: bp.y + len },
427	        ];
428	        g.lineStyle(6,   0x880000, 0.40); this._drawPolyline(g, bPts);
429	        g.lineStyle(2,   0x080000, 0.88); this._drawPolyline(g, bPts);
430	        g.lineStyle(1,   0xff3311, 0.90); this._drawPolyline(g, bPts);
431	      });
432	    };
433	
434	    drawRed();
435	    [55, 110, 165].forEach(delay => {
436	      scene.time.delayedCall(delay, () => {
437	        if (!scene.sys.isActive() || !g.active) return;
438	        drawRed();
439	      });
440	    });
441	
442	    scene.tweens.add({
443	      targets: g, alpha: 0, delay: 210, duration: 300, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
444	      onComplete: () => g.destroy(),
445	    });
446	
447	    const impact = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(308);
448	    impact.fillStyle(0xaa0000, 0.55);
449	    impact.fillCircle(tx, ty, 35);
450	    scene.tweens.add({
451	      targets: impact, scaleX: 2.2, scaleY: 2.2, alpha: 0,
452	      duration: 380, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
453	      onComplete: () => impact.destroy(),
454	    });
455	
456	    scene.time.delayedCall(180, () => {
457	      if (!scene.sys.isActive()) return;
458	      this._clearPoops(api, p => Math.abs(p.x - tx) <= 60, 10);
459	    });
460	  }
461	
462	  // ── 공통 유틸 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
463	
464	  private _clearPoops(
465	    api: GameSceneAPI,
466	    filter: (p: Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite) => boolean,
467	    pointsPerPoop: number,
468	  ): void {
469	    const { poops, scene } = api;
470	    const targets = (poops.getChildren() as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite[])
471	      .filter(p => p.active && filter(p));
472	
473	    if (targets.length === 0) return;
474	
475	    // 위치를 먼저 저장한 뒤 즉시 재활용 — 피버 타임이 얼어있는 똥을
476	    // 특수 똥으로 변환하는 race condition을 원천 차단
477	    const positions = targets.map(p => ({ x: p.x, y: p.y }));
478	    targets.forEach(p => (p as unknown as PoolablePoopBase).recycle());
479	
480	    // 잽 이펙트는 저장된 위치에서 독립적으로 재생
481	    positions.forEach(({ x, y }) => this._spawnZapEffect(x, y, scene));
482	
483	    scene.time.delayedCall(450, () => {
484	      if (!scene.sys.isActive()) return;
485	      api.addAbilityBonus(positions.length * pointsPerPoop);
486	    });
487	  }
488	
489	  private _drawPolyline(g: Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics, pts: { x: number; y: number }[]): void {
490	    g.beginPath();
491	    g.moveTo(pts[0]!.x, pts[0]!.y);
492	    for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) g.lineTo(pts[i]!.x, pts[i]!.y);
493	    g.strokePath();
494	  }
495	
496	  private _spawnZapEffect(x: number, y: number, scene: Phaser.Scene): void {
497	    const g = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(206);
498	
499	    const draw = () => {
500	      g.clear();
501	      const sparks = Phaser.Math.Between(3, 5);
502	      for (let i = 0; i < sparks; i++) {
503	        const color = Math.random() < 0.55 ? 0xffee00 : 0xffffff;
504	        g.lineStyle(Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(1.5, 2.5), color, 1);
505	        g.beginPath();
506	        let px = x + Phaser.Math.Between(-7, 7);
507	        let py = y + Phaser.Math.Between(-7, 7);
508	        g.moveTo(px, py);
509	        for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++) {
510	          px += Phaser.Math.Between(-10, 10);
511	          py += Phaser.Math.Between(-10, 10);
512	          g.lineTo(px, py);
513	        }
514	        g.strokePath();
515	      }
516	    };
517	
518	    draw();
519	    const timer = scene.time.addEvent({ delay: 80, repeat: 5, callback: draw });
520	    scene.time.delayedCall(550, () => {
521	      timer.remove();
522	      if (g.active) g.destroy();
523	    });
524	  }
525	
526	  // ── 부활 시스템 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
527	
528	  private _revive(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
529	    this.revivalCharges--;
530	    this.totalRevivalsUsed++;
531	
532	    if (this.isGoldForm) {
533	      this._resetGoldForm(api);
534	    }
535	    this._updateRevivalUI();
536	
537	    const { poops, scene } = api;
538	
539	    // 전체 똥 즉시 제거
540	    (poops.getChildren() as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite[])
541	      .filter(p => p.active)
542	      .forEach(p => (p as unknown as PoolablePoopBase).recycle());
543	
544	    // 화면 중앙으로 이동 + 연꽃 소환
545	    const centerX = scene.scale.width / 2;
546	    api.player.setX(centerX);
547	
548	    scene.cameras.main.flash(350, 255, 220, 240, true);
549	    api.player.setInvincibleBriefly(2500);
550	    this._playRevivalLotus(centerX, api.player.y, scene);
551	  }
552	
553	  private _playRevivalLotus(px: number, py: number, scene: Phaser.Scene): void {
554	    const LS = 2.2;                // 연꽃 전체 스케일
555	    const cx = px;
556	    const cy = py + 38;            // 플레이어 발 바닥 (displayH=80 → 중심 +40, base offset -2)
557	
558	    // 꽃받침
559	    const base = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(352).setScale(0);
560	    base.fillStyle(0x66bb6a, 0.85); base.fillEllipse(0, 0, 28 * LS, 9 * LS);
561	    base.fillStyle(0x81c784, 0.6);  base.fillEllipse(0, -2 * LS, 16 * LS, 5 * LS);
562	    base.setPosition(cx, cy + 2);
563	    scene.tweens.add({ targets: base, scale: 1, duration: 220, ease: 'Back.easeOut' });
564	
565	    // 금빛 중앙 글로우
566	    const glow = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(353).setScale(0);
567	    glow.fillStyle(0xffd700, 0.20); glow.fillCircle(0, 0, 30 * LS);
568	    glow.fillStyle(0xffe082, 0.35); glow.fillCircle(0, 0, 18 * LS);
569	    glow.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.55); glow.fillCircle(0, 0,  8 * LS);
570	    glow.setPosition(cx, cy - 14 * LS);
571	    scene.tweens.add({ targets: glow, scale: 1, duration: 320, ease: 'Back.easeOut' });
572	
573	    // sin(t*π) 곡선으로 양 끝 뾰족한 꽃잎 폴리곤
574	    const petalPts = (w: number, h: number): { x: number; y: number }[] => {
575	      const N = 16;
576	      const pts: { x: number; y: number }[] = [];
577	      for (let i = 0; i <= N; i++) {
578	        const t = i / N;
579	        pts.push({ x:  (w / 2) * Math.sin(t * Math.PI), y: h / 2 - t * h });
580	      }
581	      for (let i = N - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
582	        const t = i / N;
583	        pts.push({ x: -(w / 2) * Math.sin(t * Math.PI), y: h / 2 - t * h });
584	      }
585	      return pts;
586	    };
587	
588	    // 꽃잎: 바닥 한 점에서 각도 방향으로 뻗어나가는 옆면 형태
589	    const petalGfxs: Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics[] = [];
590	    LOTUS_DEFS.forEach(([rotDeg, w, h, color, depthOff], idx) => {
591	      const sw = w * LS;
592	      const sh = h * LS;
593	      const rotRad = rotDeg * Math.PI / 180;
594	      const ppx = cx + Math.sin(rotRad) * (sh / 2);
595	      const ppy = (cy + 2) - Math.cos(rotRad) * (sh / 2);
596	
597	      const g = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(353 + depthOff);
598	      g.fillStyle(color, 0.92);
599	      g.fillPoints(petalPts(sw, sh), true);
600	      g.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.45);
601	      g.fillPoints(petalPts(sw * 0.35, sh * 0.65), true);
602	      g.fillStyle(0xffd700, 0.18);
603	      g.fillEllipse(0, -(sh * 0.3), sw * 0.55, sh * 0.22);
604	      g.setPosition(ppx, ppy).setRotation(rotRad).setScale(0);
605	
606	      scene.tweens.add({ targets: g, scale: 1, delay: idx * 40, duration: 280, ease: 'Back.easeOut' });
607	      petalGfxs.push(g);
608	    });
609	
610	    // 수술
611	    const stamen = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(359).setScale(0);
612	    stamen.fillStyle(0xffd700, 1); stamen.fillCircle(0, 0, 5 * LS);
613	    stamen.fillStyle(0xffff99, 0.9); stamen.fillCircle(0, 0, 3 * LS);
614	    stamen.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.7); stamen.fillCircle(-1 * LS, -1.5 * LS, 1.5 * LS);
615	    stamen.setPosition(cx, cy - 20 * LS);
616	    scene.tweens.add({
617	      targets: stamen, scale: 1,
618	      delay: LOTUS_DEFS.length * 40 - 20, duration: 220, ease: 'Back.easeOut',
619	    });
620	
621	    // 900ms 후 조용히 페이드아웃
622	    const allObjs: Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics[] = [...petalGfxs, glow, base, stamen];
623	    scene.time.delayedCall(900, () => {
624	      if (!scene.sys.isActive()) return;
625	      allObjs.forEach(obj => {
626	        scene.tweens.add({
627	          targets: obj, alpha: 0,
628	          duration: 500, ease: 'Quad.easeIn',
629	          onComplete: () => { if (obj.active) obj.destroy(); },
630	        });
631	      });
632	    });
633	  }
634	
635	  private _playRechargeGlow(px: number, py: number, scene: Phaser.Scene): void {
636	    const g = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(320);
637	    g.lineStyle(4, 0xff88cc, 1);
638	    g.strokeCircle(px, py, 30);
639	    scene.tweens.add({
640	      targets: g, scaleX: 2.5, scaleY: 2.5, alpha: 0,
641	      duration: 500, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
642	      onComplete: () => g.destroy(),
643	    });
644	  }
645	
646	  // ── 정리 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
647	
648	  override onDestroy(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
649	    this.yeoijus.forEach(orb => orb.gfx.destroy());
650	    this.yeoijus = [];
651	    this.counterText?.destroy();
652	    this.counterText = null;
653	    this.revivalText?.destroy();
654	    this.revivalText = null;
655	    if (this.isGoldForm) {
656	      api.player.setTexturePrefix(this.normalPrefix);
657	      this.isGoldForm = false;
658	    }
659	  }
660	}

     4	
     5	/**

     9	export class NoiseAbility extends BaseAbility {

98	export class GumiAbility extends BaseAbility {
99	  // ── 여우불 (특수 능력) ─────────────────────────────────────────────
100	  private lastGumiScore = 0;

103	  // ── 꼬리 스택 (기본 효과) ─────────────────────────────────────────
104	  private tailCount = 0;
105	  private lastTailScore = 0;
106	  private invincibleActive = false;
107	  private invincibleElapsed = 0;

110	  // ── 꼬리별 누적 효과 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
111	  // index % 3 === 0 (1,4,7번): 특수 똥 수집 +5점
112	  // index % 3 === 1 (2,5,8번): 이동 속도 +5px/s (Player에 직접 적용)
113	  // index % 3 === 2 (3,6,9번): 일반 똥 소환 -1개 (영구)
114	  private specialBonus = 0;
115	  private speedBonus = 0;
116	  private spawnReduction = 0;
117	
118	  // ── 무적 오라 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
119	  private auraParticles: AuraParticle[] = [];
120	
121	  // ── 디버그 텍스트 (임시) ──────────────────────────────────────────
122	  private debugText: Phaser.GameObjects.Text | null = null;
123	
124	  // ── 플레이어 depth 설정 (꼬리가 몸통 뒤에 렌더링되도록) ──────────
125	  override onCreate(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
126	    // 씬 재시작 시 onDestroy가 호출되지 않으므로 stale 참조를 여기서 정리
127	    this.tails = [];
128	    this.tailCount = 0;
129	    this.specialBonus = 0;
130	    this.speedBonus = 0;
131	    this.spawnReduction = 0;
132	    this.debugText = null;
133	
134	    api.player.setDepth(5);
135	    this.debugText = api.scene.add.text(8, 60, '', {
136	      fontSize: '11px',
137	      color: '#ffffaa',
138	      stroke: '#000000',
139	      strokeThickness: 3,
140	      lineSpacing: 2,
141	    }).setDepth(500).setAlpha(0.85).setVisible(false);
142	    this._updateDebugText();
143	  }
144	
145	  private _updateDebugText(): void {
146	    if (!this.debugText) return;
147	    this.debugText.setText([
148	      `🦊 꼬리 ${this.tailCount}/${GUMI_PARAMS.maxTails}`,
149	      `특수+${this.specialBonus}pt  속도+${this.speedBonus}  소환-${this.spawnReduction}`,
150	    ]);
151	  }
152	
153	  // ── 꼬리 추가 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
154	  private _addTail(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
155	    const { scene, player } = api;
156	    ensureGlowDot(scene);
157	
158	    const i         = this.tailCount;
159	    const color     = TAIL_COLORS[i] ?? 0xffffff;
160	    const baseAngle = TAIL_ANGLE_ORDER[i] ?? (270 * Math.PI / 180);
161	    const originX   = player.x;
162	    const originY   = player.y + TAIL_ORIGIN_Y;
163	
164	    // 흰색(0xffffff)은 ADD 블렌드 시 RGB 3채널 전부 최대라 실제 scale보다 넓어 보임
165	    // → alpha를 낮춰 다른 색상과 시각적 균형 맞춤
166	    const segAlpha = color === 0xffffff ? 0.52 : 0.92;
167	
168	    // 먼저 생성된 꼬리(빨강 등)가 나중 꼬리(흰색 등) 위에 렌더링되도록
169	    // index가 낮을수록(앞쪽 꼬리) depth를 높게 설정
170	    const segDepth = TAIL_DEPTH + (GUMI_PARAMS.maxTails - i) * 0.1;
171	
172	    const segs: TailSegImg[] = [];
173	    for (let s = 0; s < NUM_SEGS; s++) {
174	      const img = scene.add.image(
175	        originX + Math.cos(baseAngle) * (STEP_DIST * (s + 1)),
176	        originY + Math.sin(baseAngle) * (STEP_DIST * (s + 1)),
177	        'glow_dot',
178	      )
179	        .setDepth(segDepth)
180	        .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD)
181	        .setTint(color)
182	        .setScale(0)
183	        .setAlpha(segAlpha);
184	      segs.push({ img, baseScale: SEG_SCALES[s] ?? 0.08 });
185	    }
186	
187	    // 등장 애니메이션 (base → tip 순차)
188	    segs.forEach((si, idx) => {
189	      scene.tweens.add({
190	        targets: si.img,
191	        scale: si.baseScale,
192	        delay: idx * 40,
193	        duration: 300,
194	        ease: 'Back.easeOut',
195	      });
196	    });
197	
198	    this.tails.push({
199	      segs,
200	      baseAngle,
201	      swayPhase: Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0, Math.PI * 2),
202	      swayFreq:  Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(2.2, 4.2),
203	      color,
204	    });
205	
206	    if (i % 3 === 0) {
207	      this.specialBonus += GUMI_PARAMS.tailSpecialBonus;
208	    } else if (i % 3 === 1) {
209	      this.speedBonus += GUMI_PARAMS.tailSpeedBonus;
210	      player.addPermanentSpeed(GUMI_PARAMS.tailSpeedBonus);
211	    } else {
212	      this.spawnReduction += GUMI_PARAMS.tailSpawnReduction;
213	    }
214	
215	    this.tailCount++;
216	    this._updateDebugText();
217	
218	    if (this.tailCount >= GUMI_PARAMS.maxTails) {
219	      this._triggerInvincibility(api);
220	    }
221	  }
222	
223	  // ── 꼬리 지렁이 파동 업데이트 (매 프레임) ────────────────────────
224	  private _updateTailPositions(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
225	    if (this.tails.length === 0) return;
226	    const { player, scene } = api;
227	    const time    = scene.time.now * 0.001;
228	    const originX = player.x;
229	    const originY = player.y + TAIL_ORIGIN_Y;
230	
231	    // 플레이어 속도 기반 트레일링: 오른쪽 이동 시 꼬리가 왼쪽으로 쏠림
232	    const body = player.body as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Body | null;
233	    const maxSpeed = 400;
234	    const velRatio = body ? Phaser.Math.Clamp(body.velocity.x / maxSpeed, -1, 1) : 0;
235	    const trailOffset = velRatio * TRAIL_MAX;
236	
237	    for (const tail of this.tails) {
238	      let px = originX;
239	      let py = originY;
240	
241	      for (let s = 0; s < tail.segs.length; s++) {
242	        const si = tail.segs[s];
243	        if (!si.img.active) continue;
244	
245	        // 뿌리는 진폭 0, 끝으로 갈수록 진폭 증가 (지렁이 파동)
246	        const t     = s / (NUM_SEGS - 1);
247	        const amp   = SWAY_AMP * t;
248	        const sway  = Math.sin(time * tail.swayFreq + tail.swayPhase + s * PHASE_STEP) * amp;
249	        // 트레일링: 끝 세그먼트일수록 이동 반대 방향으로 더 크게 쏠림
250	        const angle = tail.baseAngle + sway + trailOffset * t;
251	
252	        px += Math.cos(angle) * STEP_DIST;
253	        py += Math.sin(angle) * STEP_DIST;
254	        si.img.setPosition(px, py);
255	      }
256	    }
257	  }
258	
259	  // ── 점수 마일스톤 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
260	  override onScoreMilestone(score: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
261	    // 여우불 (특수)
262	    if (score % GUMI_PARAMS.foxFireInterval === 0 && score > this.lastGumiScore) {
263	      this.lastGumiScore = score;
264	      this.summonFoxFires(api);
265	    }
266	    // 꼬리 추가 (기본) — 무적 중 누적 금지
267	    if (score % GUMI_PARAMS.tailInterval === 0 && score > this.lastTailScore && !this.invincibleActive) {
268	      this.lastTailScore = score;
269	      this._addTail(api);
270	    }
271	  }
272	
273	  // ── 무적 발동 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
274	  private _triggerInvincibility(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
275	    const { player, scene } = api;
276	    this.invincibleActive  = true;
277	    this.invincibleElapsed = 0;
278	
279	    // 꼬리 확대
280	    this.tails.forEach(t => {
281	      t.segs.forEach(si => {
282	        scene.tweens.add({
283	          targets: si.img,
284	          scale: si.baseScale * 1.5,
285	          duration: 320,
286	          ease: 'Back.easeOut',
287	        });
288	      });
289	    });
290	
291	    this._createAura(api);
292	
293	    // 9색 버스트
294	    ensureGlowDot(scene);
295	    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
296	      const angle = (i / 9) * Math.PI * 2;
297	      const spark = scene.add.image(player.x, player.y, 'glow_dot')
298	        .setDepth(210)
299	        .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD)
300	        .setTint(FOX_FIRE_COLORS[i])
301	        .setScale(0.6);
302	      scene.tweens.add({
303	        targets: spark,
304	        x: player.x + Math.cos(angle) * 65,
305	        y: player.y + Math.sin(angle) * 65,
306	        scale: 0, alpha: 0, duration: 520,
307	        ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
308	        onComplete: () => spark.destroy(),
309	      });
310	    }
311	  }
312	
313	  // ── 무적 카운트다운 ───────────────────────────────────────────────
314	  private _updateInvincibility(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
315	    this.invincibleElapsed += api.scene.game.loop.delta;
316	    this._updateAura(api);
317	    if (this.invincibleElapsed >= GUMI_PARAMS.invincibleDuration) {
318	      this._endInvincibility(api);
319	    }
320	  }
321	
322	  // ── 무적 종료 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
323	  private _endInvincibility(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
324	    const { player, scene } = api;
325	
326	    this.invincibleActive = false;
327	    player.setAlpha(1);
328	
329	    // 꼬리 사라짐
330	    this.tails.forEach(t => {
331	      t.segs.forEach(si => {
332	        scene.tweens.add({
333	          targets: si.img, alpha: 0, scale: 0,
334	          duration: 380, ease: 'Quad.easeIn',
335	          onComplete: () => si.img.destroy(),
336	        });
337	      });
338	    });
339	    this.tails       = [];
340	    this.tailCount   = 0;
341	    this.lastTailScore = api.score; // 무적 중 지나친 마일스톤 역추적 방지
342	
343	    // 꼬리별 누적 효과 초기화 — speedBonus는 원복 후 초기화
344	    const speedToRemove = this.speedBonus;
345	    this.specialBonus   = 0;
346	    this.speedBonus     = 0;
347	    this.spawnReduction = 0;
348	    player.addPermanentSpeed(-speedToRemove);
349	    this._updateDebugText();
350	
351	    this._destroyAura();
352	    this._spawnBreakEffect(api);
353	    this._zapAllPoops(api);
354	  }
355	
356	  // ── 오라 생성 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
357	  private _createAura(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
358	    const { scene, player } = api;
359	    ensureGlowDot(scene);
360	    for (let i = 0; i < AURA_PARTICLE_COUNT; i++) {
361	      const angle  = (i / AURA_PARTICLE_COUNT) * Math.PI * 2;
362	      // 홀짝 번갈아 시계/반시계, 반경도 살짝 다르게
363	      const speed  = (i % 2 === 0 ? 3.2 : -2.4) + Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(-0.4, 0.4);
364	      const radius = 38 + (i % 3) * 7;
365	      const img = scene.add.image(
366	        player.x + Math.cos(angle) * radius,
367	        player.y + Math.sin(angle) * radius,
368	        'glow_dot',
369	      )
370	        .setDepth(6)
371	        .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD)
372	        .setTint(AURA_COLORS[i])
373	        .setScale(0.38)
374	        .setAlpha(0.9);
375	      this.auraParticles.push({ img, angle, speed, radius });
376	    }
377	  }
378	
379	  // ── 오라 매 프레임 업데이트 + 소각 판정 ──────────────────────────
380	  private _updateAura(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
381	    const { player, poops, scene } = api;
382	    const dt = scene.game.loop.delta * 0.001;
383	    const time = scene.time.now * 0.001;
384	
385	    for (const p of this.auraParticles) {
386	      p.angle += p.speed * dt;
387	      const x = player.x + Math.cos(p.angle) * p.radius;
388	      const y = player.y + Math.sin(p.angle) * p.radius;
389	      p.img.setPosition(x, y);
390	      // 숨쉬는 scale 펄스
391	      p.img.setScale(0.30 + Math.sin(time * 6 + p.angle) * 0.10);
392	    }
393	
394	    // 소각 판정
395	    const burnR2 = AURA_BURN_RADIUS * AURA_BURN_RADIUS;
396	    (poops.getChildren() as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite[])
397	      .filter(p => p.active
398	        && (p.x - player.x) ** 2 + (p.y - player.y) ** 2 <= burnR2)
399	      .forEach(p => {
400	        this._spawnBurnEffect(p.x, p.y, scene);
401	        (p as unknown as PoolablePoopBase).recycle();
402	      });
403	  }
404	
405	  // ── 소각 이펙트 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
406	  private _spawnBurnEffect(x: number, y: number, scene: Phaser.Scene): void {
407	    ensureGlowDot(scene);
408	    const burnColors = [0xff4400, 0xff8800, 0xffcc00];
409	    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
410	      const angle = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0, Math.PI * 2);
411	      const dist  = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(12, 26);
412	      const spark = scene.add.image(x, y, 'glow_dot')
413	        .setDepth(201)
414	        .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD)
415	        .setTint(burnColors[i % 3])
416	        .setScale(Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0.18, 0.30));
417	      scene.tweens.add({
418	        targets: spark,
419	        x: x + Math.cos(angle) * dist,
420	        y: y + Math.sin(angle) * dist - 14, // 위로 솟구치는 불꽃
421	        scale: 0,
422	        alpha: 0,
423	        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(180, 280),
424	        ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
425	        onComplete: () => spark.destroy(),
426	      });
427	    }
428	  }
429	
430	  // ── 오라 제거 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

432	    this.auraParticles.forEach(p => p.img.destroy());
433	    this.auraParticles = [];
434	  }

436	  // ── 무적 종료 파열 이펙트 (센티넬 방어막 파열 스타일, 황금색) ────
437	  private _spawnBreakEffect(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
438	    const { player, scene } = api;
439	    const cx = player.x;
440	    const cy = player.y;
441	
442	    scene.cameras.main.flash(200, 255, 200, 50, true);

444	    // 확산 링 2겹
445	    [{ delay: 0, maxScale: 4.3 }, { delay: 80, maxScale: 3.4 }].forEach(({ delay, maxScale }) => {
446	      const ring = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(150);
447	      ring.lineStyle(2.5, 0xffcc44, 0.9);
448	      ring.strokeEllipse(0, 0, 74, 98);

452	        duration: 500, delay, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',

454	      });
455	    });
456	
457	    // 내부 섬광
458	    const fill = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(149);
459	    fill.fillStyle(0xffcc44, 0.25);
460	    fill.fillEllipse(0, 0, 74, 98);

462	    scene.tweens.add({
463	      targets: fill, alpha: 0, duration: 180, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
464	      onComplete: () => fill.destroy(),
465	    });
466	
467	    // 황금 파편 8개
468	    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
469	      const angle = (i / 8) * Math.PI * 2 + Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(-0.25, 0.25);
470	      const speed = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(40, 95);
471	      const w     = Phaser.Math.Between(5, 12);
472	      const h     = Phaser.Math.Between(2, 5);

477	      shard.setRotation(angle);
478	      scene.tweens.add({
479	        targets: shard,
480	        x: cx + Math.cos(angle) * speed,
481	        y: cy + Math.sin(angle) * speed,
482	        alpha: 0, rotation: angle + Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(-1, 1),
483	        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(270, 440), ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
484	        onComplete: () => shard.destroy(),
485	      });
486	    }
487	  }

542	  // ── 여우불 소환 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
543	  private summonFoxFires(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
544	    const { scene, player } = api;
545	    const N = GUMI_PARAMS.foxFireCount;
546	    ensureGlowDot(scene);
547	
548	    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
549	      const ringAngle = (i / N) * Math.PI * 2;
550	      const x = player.x + Math.cos(ringAngle) * 55;
551	      const y = player.y + Math.sin(ringAngle) * 55;
552	      const color = FOX_FIRE_COLORS[i];
553	
554	      const img = scene.add.image(x, y, 'glow_dot')
555	        .setDepth(200)
556	        .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD)
557	        .setTint(color)
558	        .setScale(0.45);
559	
560	      const core = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(202);
561	      core.fillStyle(0xffffff, 1);
562	      core.fillCircle(0, 0, 5);
563	      core.fillStyle(color, 0.85);
564	      core.fillCircle(0, 0, 3.5);
565	      core.setPosition(x, y);
566	
567	      this.foxFires.push({
568	        img, core, x, y, color,
569	        baseVx:     Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(-3.5, 3.5),
570	        speedY:     Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(4.5, 7.5),
571	        phase:      Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0, Math.PI * 2),
572	        freq:       Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0.07, 0.17),
573	        trailTimer: 0,
574	      });
575	    }
576	  }
577	
578	  // ── 매 프레임 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
579	  override onUpdate(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
580	    this._updateTailPositions(api);
581	    if (this.invincibleActive) this._updateInvincibility(api);
582	
583	    if (this.foxFires.length === 0) return;
584	
585	    const { scene } = api;
586	    const activePoops = api.poops.getChildren().filter(
587	      p => (p as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite).active,
588	    ) as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite[];
589	
590	    const remaining: FoxFire[] = [];
591	
592	    for (const fire of this.foxFires) {
593	      fire.trailTimer++;
594	      fire.x += fire.baseVx + Math.sin(fire.phase + fire.trailTimer * fire.freq) * 6;
595	      fire.y -= fire.speedY;
596	      fire.img.setPosition(fire.x, fire.y);
597	      fire.core.setPosition(fire.x, fire.y);
598	
599	      if (fire.trailTimer % 3 === 0) {
600	        const trail = scene.add.image(fire.x, fire.y, 'glow_dot')
601	          .setDepth(199)
602	          .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD)
603	          .setTint(fire.color)
604	          .setScale(0.22);
605	        scene.tweens.add({
606	          targets: trail, scale: 0, alpha: 0, duration: 200,
607	          ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
608	          onComplete: () => trail.destroy(),
609	        });
610	      }
611	
612	      if (fire.y < -30 || fire.x < -30 || fire.x > 430) {
613	        fire.img.destroy();
614	        fire.core.destroy();
615	        continue;
616	      }
617	
618	      let hit = false;
619	      for (const poop of activePoops) {
620	        const dx = fire.x - poop.x;
621	        const dy = fire.y - poop.y;
622	        if (dx * dx + dy * dy < HIT_R * HIT_R) {
623	          const px = poop.x;
624	          const py = poop.y;
625	          (poop as unknown as PoolablePoopBase).recycle();
626	          api.spawnGoldPoopAt(px, py);
627	          this.spawnHitFlash(px, py, scene);
628	          fire.img.destroy();
629	          fire.core.destroy();
630	          hit = true;
631	          break;
632	        }
633	      }
634	
635	      if (!hit) remaining.push(fire);
636	    }
637	
638	    this.foxFires = remaining;
639	  }
640	
641	  private spawnHitFlash(x: number, y: number, scene: Phaser.Scene): void {
642	    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
643	      const angle = (i / 6) * Math.PI * 2;
644	      const spark = scene.add.image(x, y, 'glow_dot')
645	        .setDepth(201)
646	        .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD)
647	        .setTint(0xffd700)
648	        .setScale(0.3);
649	      scene.tweens.add({
650	        targets: spark,
651	        x: x + Math.cos(angle) * 25,
652	        y: y + Math.sin(angle) * 25,
653	        scale: 0, alpha: 0, duration: 300,
654	        ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
655	        onComplete: () => spark.destroy(),
656	      });
657	    }
658	  }
659	
660	  override onDestroy(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
661	    if (this.invincibleActive) {
662	      api.player.setAlpha(1);
663	    }
664	    api.player.setDepth(0); // player depth 원복
665	    this.foxFires.forEach(f => { f.img.destroy(); f.core.destroy(); });
666	    this.foxFires = [];
667	    this.tails.forEach(t => t.segs.forEach(si => si.img.destroy()));
668	    this.tails = [];
669	    this._destroyAura();
670	    this.debugText?.destroy();
671	    this.debugText = null;
672	  }
673	}

9	 * ★1: 8초 / 35%/2개 65%/4개 / 450점마다 / 10초 / 1.25배

12	 */
13	export class LegacyAbility extends BaseAbility {

376	  private burnRandomPoops(api: GameSceneAPI, count: number): void {
377	    const visible = api.poops.getChildren().filter(
378	      c => c.active && (c as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite).y > -10,
379	    ) as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite[];
380	
381	    if (visible.length === 0) return;
382	
383	    // Partial Fisher-Yates: count개만 앞으로 뽑아내고 나머지 순회 생략
384	    const pool = visible.slice();
385	    const take = Math.min(count, pool.length);
386	    for (let i = 0; i < take; i++) {
387	      const j = i + Math.floor(Math.random() * (pool.length - i));
388	      [pool[i], pool[j]] = [pool[j], pool[i]];
389	    }
390	    const targets = pool.slice(0, take);
391	
392	    this.drawFireTrail(
393	      api.scene,
394	      api.player.x, api.player.y,
395	      targets.map(p => ({ x: p.x, y: p.y })),
396	    );
397	
398	    for (const poop of targets) {
399	      if (!poop.active) continue;
400	      this.playBurnEffect(api.scene, poop.x, poop.y);
401	      (poop as PoolablePoopBase).recycle();
402	      api.addAbilityBonus(10);
403	    }
404	  }

1	import Phaser from 'phaser';
2	import { BaseAbility } from './BaseAbility';
3	import type { GameSceneAPI } from './types';
4	import type PoolablePoopBase from '../objects/PoolablePoopBase';
5	import { KNIGHT_PARAMS } from '../config/abilityParams';
6	import { ensureGlowDot } from '../utils/glowDot';
7	
8	const R_OUT  = 26;
9	const R_IN   = 20;
10	const OFFSET = 12;
11	const STEPS  = 30;
12	
13	interface WaveCfg { scale: number; speed: number; amp: number; phase: number; }
14	
15	// 형상이 고정이므로 모듈 초기화 시 한 번만 계산
16	const BASE_PTS: Phaser.Geom.Point[] = (() => {
17	  const tipY = (R_OUT * R_OUT - R_IN * R_IN + OFFSET * OFFSET) / (2 * OFFSET);
18	  const tipX = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, R_OUT * R_OUT - tipY * tipY));
19	  const outerStart = Math.atan2(tipY, tipX);
20	  const outerSpan  = -(2 * Math.PI - (Math.atan2(tipY, -tipX) - outerStart));
21	  const innerStart = Math.atan2(tipY - OFFSET, -tipX);
22	  const innerSpan  = 2 * Math.PI - (innerStart - Math.atan2(tipY - OFFSET, tipX));
23	  const pts: Phaser.Geom.Point[] = [];
24	  for (let i = 0; i <= STEPS; i++) {
25	    const a = outerStart + outerSpan * (i / STEPS);
26	    pts.push(new Phaser.Geom.Point(Math.cos(a) * R_OUT, Math.sin(a) * R_OUT));
27	  }
28	  for (let i = 0; i <= STEPS; i++) {
29	    const a = innerStart + innerSpan * (i / STEPS);
30	    pts.push(new Phaser.Geom.Point(Math.cos(a) * R_IN, Math.sin(a) * R_IN + OFFSET));
31	  }
32	  return pts;
33	})();
34	
35	// 프레임당 재사용할 Point 버퍼 — new Point() 할당 없이 in-place 덮어쓰기
36	// JS는 단일 스레드 + tween onUpdate 순차 실행이므로 레이어별 버퍼 1개로 충분
37	const _bufs = {
38	  outer: BASE_PTS.map(() => new Phaser.Geom.Point()),
39	  mid:   BASE_PTS.map(() => new Phaser.Geom.Point()),
40	  body:  BASE_PTS.map(() => new Phaser.Geom.Point()),
41	};
42	
43	export class KnightAbility extends BaseAbility {
44	  private lastBeamScore = 0;
45	
46	  // onDestroy 시 정리할 리소스 추적
47	  private activeTimers: Phaser.Time.TimerEvent[]       = [];
48	  private activeGraphics: Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics[] = [];
49	
50	  override onScoreMilestone(score: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
51	    if (score % KNIGHT_PARAMS.beamInterval === 0 && score > this.lastBeamScore) {
52	      this.lastBeamScore = score;
53	      [-30, 0, 30].forEach(deg => this.fireSingleCrescent(api, deg));
54	    }
55	  }
56	
57	  override onDestroy(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
58	    this.activeTimers.forEach(t => t.destroy());
59	    this.activeTimers = [];
60	    this.activeGraphics.forEach(g => {
61	      api.scene.tweens.killTweensOf(g);
62	      if (g.active) g.destroy();
63	    });
64	    this.activeGraphics = [];
65	  }
66	
67	  private wavyPoints(buf: Phaser.Geom.Point[], time: number, cfg: WaveCfg): Phaser.Geom.Point[] {
68	    BASE_PTS.forEach((p, i) => {
69	      const r = Math.hypot(p.x, p.y);
70	      const wave = Math.sin(time * cfg.speed + i * 0.32 + cfg.phase) * cfg.amp;
71	      const s = r > 0.1 ? (r * cfg.scale + wave) / r : cfg.scale;
72	      buf[i].x = p.x * s;
73	      buf[i].y = p.y * s;
74	    });
75	    return buf;
76	  }
77	
78	  private fireSingleCrescent(api: GameSceneAPI, rotationDeg: number): void {
79	    const scene  = api.scene;
80	    const px     = api.player.x;
81	    const py     = api.player.y - 20;
82	    const rotRad = rotationDeg * (Math.PI / 180);
83	    const dx     = Math.sin(rotRad);
84	    const dy     = -Math.cos(rotRad);
85	    const TRAVEL = py + 80;
86	    const duration = (TRAVEL / 520) * 1000;
87	
88	    const makeGfx = (depth: number): Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics => {
89	      const g = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(depth).setPosition(px, py).setRotation(rotRad);
90	      this.activeGraphics.push(g);
91	      return g;
92	    };
93	
94	    const outerGlow = makeGfx(118);
95	    const midGlow   = makeGfx(119);
96	    const body      = makeGfx(120);
97	    const edge      = makeGfx(121);
98	    const all       = [outerGlow, midGlow, body, edge];
99	
100	    const particleTimer = scene.time.addEvent({
101	      delay: 45,
102	      loop: true,
103	      callback: () => {
104	        if (!body.active) return;
105	        const bp = BASE_PTS[Phaser.Math.Between(0, BASE_PTS.length - 1)];
106	        const cos = Math.cos(rotRad), sin = Math.sin(rotRad);
107	        const wx = body.x + bp.x * cos - bp.y * sin;
108	        const wy = body.y + bp.x * sin + bp.y * cos;
109	        this.spawnSpark(scene, wx, wy, {
110	          color: Phaser.Math.Between(0, 1) ? 0xff9900 : 0xffcc44,
111	          radius: Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(1, 3),
112	          tx: wx + Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(-12, 12),
113	          ty: wy + Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(-18, 4),
114	          duration: Phaser.Math.Between(180, 320),
115	          endScale: 0.2,
116	        });
117	      },
118	    });
119	    this.activeTimers.push(particleTimer);
120	
121	    const hitSet = new Set<Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite>();
122	    const HIT_RADIUS = R_OUT + 10;
123	
124	    scene.tweens.add({
125	      targets: all,
126	      x: `+=${dx * TRAVEL}`,
127	      y: `+=${dy * TRAVEL}`,
128	      duration,
129	      ease: 'Linear',
130	      onUpdate: () => {
131	        const t  = scene.time.now;
132	        outerGlow.clear();
133	        outerGlow.fillStyle(0xff6600, 0.12 + Math.sin(t * 0.011) * 0.07);
134	        outerGlow.fillPoints(this.wavyPoints(_bufs.outer, t, { scale: 1.65, speed: 0.007, amp: 5.5, phase: 0 }), true);
135	
136	        midGlow.clear();
137	        midGlow.fillStyle(0xff8800, 0.28 + Math.sin(t * 0.014 + 1.2) * 0.1);
138	        midGlow.fillPoints(this.wavyPoints(_bufs.mid, t, { scale: 1.25, speed: 0.01, amp: 3.5, phase: 1.0 }), true);
139	
140	        const bodyPts = this.wavyPoints(_bufs.body, t, { scale: 1.0, speed: 0.013, amp: 2.5, phase: 2.1 });
141	
142	        body.clear();
143	        body.fillStyle(0xffaa44, 0.92);
144	        body.fillPoints(bodyPts, true);
145	
146	        // body와 동일 파형 → 캐시된 bodyPts 재사용
147	        edge.clear();
148	        edge.lineStyle(2, 0xffeecc, 0.9);
149	        edge.strokePoints(bodyPts, true);
150	
151	        const cx = body.x, cy = body.y;
152	        (api.poops.getChildren() as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite[]).forEach(sp => {
153	          if (!sp.active || hitSet.has(sp)) return;
154	          if (Phaser.Math.Distance.Between(cx, cy, sp.x, sp.y) <= HIT_RADIUS) {
155	            hitSet.add(sp);
156	            for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
157	              const angle = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0, Math.PI * 2);
158	              const dist  = Phaser.Math.Between(10, 28);
159	              this.spawnSpark(scene, sp.x, sp.y, {
160	                color: 0xffaa55,
161	                radius: Phaser.Math.Between(2, 5),
162	                tx: sp.x + Math.cos(angle) * dist,
163	                ty: sp.y + Math.sin(angle) * dist - 8,
164	                duration: Phaser.Math.Between(180, 380),
165	              });
166	            }
167	            (sp as PoolablePoopBase).recycle();
168	            api.addAbilityBonus(KNIGHT_PARAMS.beamKillBonus);
169	          }
170	        });
171	      },
172	      onComplete: () => {
173	        particleTimer.destroy();
174	        this.activeTimers = this.activeTimers.filter(t => t !== particleTimer);
175	        all.forEach(g => {
176	          this.activeGraphics = this.activeGraphics.filter(ag => ag !== g);
177	          g.destroy();
178	        });
179	      },
180	    });
181	  }
182	
183	  private spawnSpark(
184	    scene: Phaser.Scene,
185	    x: number, y: number,
186	    opts: { color: number; radius: number; tx: number; ty: number; duration: number; endScale?: number },
187	  ): void {
188	    ensureGlowDot(scene);
189	    const initScale = opts.radius / 10;
190	    const img = scene.add.image(x, y, 'glow_dot')
191	      .setDepth(122)
192	      .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD)
193	      .setTint(opts.color)
194	      .setScale(initScale);
195	
196	    const tween: Phaser.Types.Tweens.TweenBuilderConfig = {
197	      targets: img, x: opts.tx, y: opts.ty, alpha: 0,
198	      duration: opts.duration, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
199	      onComplete: () => img.destroy(),
200	    };
201	    if (opts.endScale !== undefined) {
202	      (tween as Record<string, unknown>).scale = opts.endScale * initScale;
203	    }
204	    scene.tweens.add(tween);
205	  }
206	}
207

export const LEGACY_PARAMS = {
  feverDuration: 6000,        // 시작 피버 지속 시간 (ms)
  legacyInterval: 600,        // 레거시 모드 발동 점수 간격
  legacyDuration: 6000,      // 레거시 모드 지속 시간 (ms)
  scoreExtra: 0.20,           // 레거시 모드 점수 추가분 (1.2배)

  burnChanceLegacy: 0.90,     // 레거시 불태우기 확률
  burnCountNormal: 2,         // 일반 불태우기 개수
  burnCountLegacy: 4,         // 레거시 불태우기 개수
} as const;

export const LEGACY_DESC = {
  basicEffect: `시작 ${LEGACY_PARAMS.feverDuration / 1000}초 금똥 피버 + 황금 빗줄기 / 스폰마다 ${LEGACY_PARAMS.burnChanceNormal * 100}% 확률로 똥 ${LEGACY_PARAMS.burnCountNormal}개 불태워 소멸 (+10점)`,
  specialAbility: `${LEGACY_PARAMS.legacyInterval}점마다 ${LEGACY_PARAMS.legacyDuration / 1000}초간 레거시 모드: 점수 ${1 + LEGACY_PARAMS.scoreExtra}배 · 황금 빗줄기 강화 · 불태우기 ${LEGACY_PARAMS.burnChanceLegacy * 100}% 확률 ${LEGACY_PARAMS.burnCountLegacy}개 소멸`,
} as const;

1	import { BaseAbility } from './BaseAbility';
2	import type { GameSceneAPI } from './types';
3	import type PoolablePoopBase from '../objects/PoolablePoopBase';
4	import { LEGACY_PARAMS } from '../config/abilityParams';

6	/**
7	 * 레거시 (UR) — 피버 + 레거시 모드
8	 * ★0: 6초 피버 / 불태우기 30%/2개(일반) 60%/4개(레거시) / 500점마다 / 10초 / 1.2배
9	 * ★1: 8초 / 35%/2개 65%/4개 / 450점마다 / 10초 / 1.25배
10	 * ★2: 10초 / 40%/3개 70%/4개 / 400점마다 / 12초 / 1.30배
11	 * ★3: 12초 / 45%/3개 75%/4개 / 350점마다 / 15초 / 1.35배 + 모드 종료 직후 3초 미니 피버
12	 */
13	export class LegacyAbility extends BaseAbility {
14	  private startFeverActive = false;
15	  private legacyModeActive = false;
16	  private accum           = 0;
17	  private lastLegacyScore = 0;
18	
19	  private legacyTopGlow?:       Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics;
20	  private legacyPulseTween?:    Phaser.Tweens.Tween;
21	  private legacyRainTimer?:     Phaser.Time.TimerEvent;
22	  private legacyEndTimer?:      Phaser.Time.TimerEvent; // activateLegacyMode 종료 delayedCall
23	  private startFeverTimer?:     Phaser.Time.TimerEvent; // startFeverRain addEvent
24	  private startFeverEndTimer?:  Phaser.Time.TimerEvent; // onCreate 피버 종료 delayedCall
25	
26	  // A: 상시 불꽃 오라
27	  private oraTimer = 0;
28	  private static readonly ORA_INTERVAL_NORMAL = 180; // ms
29	  private static readonly ORA_INTERVAL_LEGACY =  80; // ms
30	  private static readonly ORA_COLS_NORMAL = [0xff3300, 0xff6600, 0xffaa00];
31	  private static readonly ORA_COLS_LEGACY = [0xffaa00, 0xffcc00, 0xffee44, 0xffffff];
32	
33	  // D: 빗줄기 색상
34	  private static readonly RAIN_COLS_FEVER  = [0xff8800, 0xffaa00, 0xffcc00, 0xffee00];
35	  private static readonly RAIN_COLS_LEGACY = [0xff5500, 0xff8800, 0xffaa00, 0xffcc00];
36	
37	  // ── 불꽃 물방울 다각형 좌표 ─────────────────────────────────────────
38	  private static flamePts(w: number, h: number) {
39	    return [
40	      { x:  0,         y: -h        },
41	      { x:  w * 0.55,  y: -h * 0.1  },
42	      { x:  w * 0.75,  y:  h * 0.35 },
43	      { x:  w * 0.3,   y:  h        },
44	      { x: -w * 0.3,   y:  h        },
45	      { x: -w * 0.75,  y:  h * 0.35 },
46	      { x: -w * 0.55,  y: -h * 0.1  },
47	    ];
48	  }
49	
50	  // ── 8각 별 좌표 (빗줄기 공용) ────────────────────────────────────────
51	  private static starPts(s: number) {
52	    return [
53	      { x:  0,       y: -s       }, { x:  s * 0.4, y: -s * 0.4 },
54	      { x:  s,       y:  0       }, { x:  s * 0.4, y:  s * 0.4 },
55	      { x:  0,       y:  s       }, { x: -s * 0.4, y:  s * 0.4 },
56	      { x: -s,       y:  0       }, { x: -s * 0.4, y: -s * 0.4 },
57	    ];
58	  }
59	
60	  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
61	  // 생명주기
62	  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
63	
64	  override onCreate(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
65	    this.startFeverActive = true;
66	    this.startFeverRain(api);
67	    this.startFeverEndTimer = api.scene.time.delayedCall(LEGACY_PARAMS.feverDuration, () => {
68	      this.startFeverActive = false;
69	    });
70	  }
71	
72	  override onUpdate(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
73	    const delta = api.scene.game.loop.delta;
74	    this.oraTimer += delta;
75	    const interval = this.legacyModeActive
76	      ? LegacyAbility.ORA_INTERVAL_LEGACY
77	      : LegacyAbility.ORA_INTERVAL_NORMAL;
78	    if (this.oraTimer >= interval) {
79	      this.oraTimer -= interval;
80	      this.spawnOraFlame(api);
81	    }
82	  }
83	
84	  override getTickScore(base: number): number {
85	    if (!this.legacyModeActive) return base;
86	    this.accum += base * LEGACY_PARAMS.scoreExtra;
87	    const bonus = Math.floor(this.accum);
88	    this.accum -= bonus;
89	    return base + bonus;
90	  }
91	
92	  override overrideSpawnPoop(api: GameSceneAPI): boolean {
93	    if (this.startFeverActive) {
94	      api.spawnGoldPoop();
95	      api.spawnGoldPoop();
96	      return true;
97	    }
98	    return false;
99	  }
100	
101	  override onAfterSpawnPoop(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
102	    const chance = this.legacyModeActive ? LEGACY_PARAMS.burnChanceLegacy : LEGACY_PARAMS.burnChanceNormal;
103	    const count  = this.legacyModeActive ? LEGACY_PARAMS.burnCountLegacy  : LEGACY_PARAMS.burnCountNormal;
104	    if (Math.random() < chance) {
105	      this.burnRandomPoops(api, count);
106	    }
107	  }
108	
109	  override onScoreMilestone(score: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
110	    if (score % LEGACY_PARAMS.legacyInterval === 0 && score > this.lastLegacyScore) {
111	      this.lastLegacyScore = score;
112	      this.activateLegacyMode(api);
113	    }
114	  }
115

117	  // A: 상시 불꽃 오라

120	  private spawnOraFlame(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
121	    const scene = api.scene;
122	    const px    = api.player.x;
123	    const py    = api.player.y;
124	    const leg   = this.legacyModeActive;
125	    const cols  = leg ? LegacyAbility.ORA_COLS_LEGACY : LegacyAbility.ORA_COLS_NORMAL;
126	    const count = leg ? Phaser.Math.Between(5, 7) : Phaser.Math.Between(2, 3);
127	    const rMax  = leg ? 2.2  : 1.6;
128	    const aMax  = leg ? 0.90 : 0.70;
129	    const riseMin = leg ? 20  : 14;
130	    const riseMax = leg ? 42  : 28;
131	    const lifeMin = leg ? 280 : 180;
132	    const lifeMax = leg ? 520 : 380;
133	
134	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
135	      const gfx = scene.add.graphics();
136	      const r   = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0.8, rMax);
137	      const col = Phaser.Utils.Array.GetRandom(cols) as number;
138	      const a   = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0.50, aMax);
139	
140	      gfx.fillStyle(col, a);
141	      gfx.fillCircle(0, 0, r);
142	      gfx.setPosition(
143	        px + Phaser.Math.Between(-10, 10),
144	        py + Phaser.Math.Between(10, 24),
145	      );
146	      gfx.setDepth(88);
147	
148	      scene.tweens.add({
149	        targets:  gfx,
150	        y:        gfx.y - Phaser.Math.Between(riseMin, riseMax),
151	        x:        gfx.x + Phaser.Math.Between(-4, 4),
152	        alpha:    0,
153	        scaleX:   0.15,
154	        scaleY:   0.15,
155	        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(lifeMin, lifeMax),
156	        ease:     'Quad.easeOut',
157	        onComplete: () => { gfx.destroy(); },
158	      });
159	    }
160	  }
161	
162	  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
163	  // B: 화염 추적선
164	  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
165	
166	  private drawFireTrail(
167	    scene: Phaser.Scene,
168	    fromX: number, fromY: number,
169	    toList: { x: number; y: number }[],
170	  ): void {
171	    if (toList.length === 0) return;
172	
173	    const gfx = scene.add.graphics();

180	      gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(fromX, fromY); gfx.lineTo(x, y); gfx.strokePath();
181	    }
182	
183	    scene.tweens.add({
184	      targets: gfx, alpha: 0, duration: 200, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
185	      onComplete: () => { gfx.destroy(); },
186	    });
187	  }

193	  private activateLegacyMode(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
194	    if (this.legacyModeActive) return;
195	    this.legacyModeActive = true;
196	    this.accum = 0;

211	
212	    // ─ 팽창 링 2개 ───────────────────────────────────────────────
213	    [{ delay: 0, maxScale: 4.3 }, { delay: 80, maxScale: 3.4 }].forEach(({ delay, maxScale }) => {

224	
225	    // ─ 채움 타원 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
226	    const fill = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(154);
227	    fill.fillStyle(0xffaa00, 0.28);
228	    fill.fillEllipse(0, 0, 74, 98);
229	    fill.setPosition(cx, cy);
230	    scene.tweens.add({
231	      targets: fill, alpha: 0, duration: 180, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
232	      onComplete: () => { fill.destroy(); },
233	    });
234	
235	    // ─ 파편 8개 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
236	    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
237	      const angle = (i / 8) * Math.PI * 2 + Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(-0.25, 0.25);
238	      const speed = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(40, 95);
239	      const sw    = Phaser.Math.Between(5, 12);
240	      const sh    = Phaser.Math.Between(2, 5);
241	      const shard = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(156);
242	      shard.fillStyle(0xffee00, 1);
243	      shard.fillRect(-sw / 2, -sh / 2, sw, sh);
244	      shard.setPosition(cx, cy).setRotation(angle);
245	      scene.tweens.add({
246	        targets: shard,
247	        x: cx + Math.cos(angle) * speed, y: cy + Math.sin(angle) * speed,
248	        alpha: 0, rotation: angle + Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(-1, 1),
249	        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(270, 440), ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
250	        onComplete: () => { shard.destroy(); },
251	      });
252	    }
253	
254	    // ─ 화면 전체 불꽃 폭발 ────────────────────────────────
255	    const BURST_COLS = [0xff2200, 0xff6600, 0xffaa00, 0xffdd00, 0xffff88];
256	    for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
257	      const gfx = scene.add.graphics();
258	      const w   = Phaser.Math.Between(4, 10);
259	      const h   = Phaser.Math.Between(10, 22);

261	
262	      gfx.fillStyle(col, Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0.22, 0.50));

264	      gfx.fillStyle(0xffee88, Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0.28, 0.55));
265	      gfx.fillPoints(LegacyAbility.flamePts(w * 0.4, h * 0.5), true);
266	
267	      gfx.setPosition(Phaser.Math.Between(20, scene.scale.width - 20), Phaser.Math.Between(40, scene.scale.height - 40));
268	      gfx.setRotation(Phaser.Math.DegToRad(Phaser.Math.Between(-180, 180)));
269	      gfx.setDepth(158).setAlpha(0);
270	
271	      scene.tweens.add({
272	        targets: gfx, alpha: 1,
273	        scaleX: Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(1.2, 1.9),
274	        scaleY: Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(1.2, 1.9),
275	        duration: 140, delay: Phaser.Math.Between(0, 280), ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
276	        onComplete: () => {
277	          scene.tweens.add({
278	            targets: gfx, alpha: 0, y: gfx.y - Phaser.Math.Between(20, 50),
279	            duration: Phaser.Math.Between(380, 650), ease: 'Quad.easeIn',
280	            onComplete: () => { gfx.destroy(); },
281	          });
282	        },
283	      });
284	    }
285	
286	    // ─ 상단 노란빛 그라데이션 ────────────────────────────────────
287	    this.legacyTopGlow = scene.add.graphics();
288	    this.legacyTopGlow.fillGradientStyle(0xffee00, 0xffee00, 0xffee00, 0xffee00, 0.28, 0.28, 0, 0);
289	    this.legacyTopGlow.fillRect(0, 0, scene.scale.width, Math.round(scene.scale.height * 0.37));
290	    this.legacyTopGlow.setDepth(148).setScrollFactor(0);
291	
292	    this.legacyPulseTween = scene.tweens.add({
293	      targets: [this.legacyTopGlow], alpha: 0.55,
294	      duration: 600, yoyo: true, repeat: -1, ease: 'Sine.easeInOut',
295	    });
296	
297	    // ─ 레거시 모드 황금 빗줄기 (repeat: -1, 10초 후 remove로 정리) ─
298	    this.legacyRainTimer = scene.time.addEvent({
299	      delay: 120,
300	      repeat: -1,
301	      callback: () => { this.spawnRainStar(scene, LegacyAbility.RAIN_COLS_LEGACY, 0.75, 1.00, 3); },
302	    });
303	
304	    // 레거시 모드 종료
305	    this.legacyEndTimer = scene.time.delayedCall(LEGACY_PARAMS.legacyDuration, () => {
306	      this.legacyModeActive = false;
307	      this.legacyRainTimer?.remove();
308	      this.legacyRainTimer = undefined;
309	      this.legacyPulseTween?.stop();
310	      this.legacyPulseTween = undefined;
311	      scene.tweens.add({
312	        targets: [this.legacyTopGlow], alpha: 0, duration: 400, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
313	        onComplete: () => {
314	          this.legacyTopGlow?.destroy();
315	          this.legacyTopGlow = undefined;
316	        },
317	      });
318	
319	    });
320	  }

323	  // D: 황금 빗줄기

326	  private startFeverRain(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
327	    const scene  = api.scene;
328	    const REPEAT = Math.floor(LEGACY_PARAMS.feverDuration / 140) - 1;
329	
330	    this.startFeverTimer = scene.time.addEvent({
331	      delay: 140,
332	      repeat: REPEAT,
333	      callback: () => { this.spawnRainStar(scene, LegacyAbility.RAIN_COLS_FEVER, 0.60, 0.90, 2); },
334	    });

337	  /** 빗줄기 별 1개 생성 — startFeverRain / legacyRainTimer 공용 */
338	  private spawnRainStar(
339	    scene: Phaser.Scene,
340	    cols: readonly number[],
341	    aMin: number,
342	    aMax: number,
343	    count: number,
344	  ): void {
345	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
346	      const gfx = scene.add.graphics();
347	      const s   = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(2.5, 5.5);
348	      const col = Phaser.Utils.Array.GetRandom(cols as number[]) as number;
349	
350	      gfx.fillStyle(col, Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(aMin, aMax));
351	      gfx.fillPoints(LegacyAbility.starPts(s), true);
352	      gfx.fillStyle(0xffffff, aMax > 0.85 ? 0.65 : 0.50); // 레거시 모드는 하이라이트도 강하게
353	      gfx.fillCircle(0, 0, s * 0.32);
354	
355	      gfx.setPosition(Phaser.Math.Between(10, scene.scale.width - 10), -15);
356	      gfx.setRotation(Phaser.Math.DegToRad(Phaser.Math.Between(0, 360)));
357	      gfx.setDepth(85);
358	
359	      scene.tweens.add({
360	        targets:  gfx,
361	        y:        gfx.y + Phaser.Math.Between(450, 600),
362	        x:        gfx.x + Phaser.Math.Between(-25, 25),
363	        rotation: gfx.rotation + Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(1.5, 3.0),
364	        alpha:    0,
365	        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(2200, 3600),
366	        ease:     'Linear',
367	        onComplete: () => { gfx.destroy(); },
368	      });
369	    }
370	  }

373	  // 불태우기 로직

376	  private burnRandomPoops(api: GameSceneAPI, count: number): void {
377	    const visible = api.poops.getChildren().filter(
378	      c => c.active && (c as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite).y > -10,
379	    ) as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite[];
380	
381	    if (visible.length === 0) return;
382	
383	    // Partial Fisher-Yates: count개만 앞으로 뽑아내고 나머지 순회 생략
384	    const pool = visible.slice();
385	    const take = Math.min(count, pool.length);
386	    for (let i = 0; i < take; i++) {
387	      const j = i + Math.floor(Math.random() * (pool.length - i));
388	      [pool[i], pool[j]] = [pool[j], pool[i]];
389	    }
390	    const targets = pool.slice(0, take);
391	
392	    this.drawFireTrail(
393	      api.scene,
394	      api.player.x, api.player.y,
395	      targets.map(p => ({ x: p.x, y: p.y })),
396	    );
397	
398	    for (const poop of targets) {
399	      if (!poop.active) continue;
400	      this.playBurnEffect(api.scene, poop.x, poop.y);
401	      (poop as PoolablePoopBase).recycle();
402	      api.addAbilityBonus(10);
403	    }
404	  }

414	    this.legacyRainTimer?.remove();
415	    this.legacyPulseTween?.stop();
416	    this.legacyTopGlow?.destroy();
417	  }

433

441	      gfx.fillStyle(0xffcc00, 0.68); gfx.fillPoints(LegacyAbility.flamePts(w * 0.40, h * 0.50), true);

461	          });
462	        },
463	      });

474	      gfx.fillStyle(0xffee88, 0.65);

44	  override onCreate(api: GameSceneAPI): void {

74	  override onScoreMilestone(score: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
75	    const interval = this.isGoldForm ? MUGI_PARAMS.yeoijuGoldInterval : MUGI_PARAMS.yeoijuInterval;
76	    if (score % interval === 0 && score > this.lastYeoijuScore) {
77	      this.lastYeoijuScore = score;

106	    gfx.fillStyle(0xdd2200, 0.80); gfx.fillCircle(1, 2, 5);
107	    gfx.fillStyle(0xff5500, 0.55); gfx.fillCircle(1, 3, 2.5);
108	    // 반사 하이라이트 — 왼쪽 위 (구슬 3D 효과)

110	    gfx.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.40); gfx.fillCircle(-2.5, -5, 1);
111	    gfx.setPosition(x, -20);
112

121	  private _updateYeoijus(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
122	    const delta = api.scene.game.loop.delta * 0.001;
123	    const { player } = api;
124	    const fallDelta = (api.baseSpeed + api.difficultyLevel * 40) * delta;
125	    const r2 = MUGI_PARAMS.yeoijuCollectRadius ** 2;
126	    const remaining: YeoijuOrb[] = [];
127	
128	    for (const orb of this.yeoijus) {
129	      orb.y += fallDelta;
130	      orb.gfx.setPosition(orb.x, orb.y);
131	
132	      const dx = player.x - orb.x;
133	      const dy = player.y - orb.y;
134	      if (dx * dx + dy * dy < r2) {
135	        this._collectYeoiju(orb, api);
136	        continue;
137	      }
138	      if (orb.y > 660) { orb.gfx.destroy(); continue; }
139	      remaining.push(orb);
140	    }
141	    this.yeoijus = remaining;
142	  }
143	
144	  private _collectYeoiju(orb: YeoijuOrb, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
145	    this._spawnCollectEffect(orb.x, orb.y, api.scene);
146	    orb.gfx.destroy();
147	    api.addAbilityBonus(80);
148	
149	    // Revival recharge: runs regardless of gold form
150	    if (this.revivalCharges === 0 && this.totalRevivalsUsed < 3) {
151	      this.revivalChargeCount++;
152	      if (this.revivalChargeCount >= MUGI_PARAMS.goldThreshold) {
153	        this.revivalCharges = 1;
154	        this.revivalChargeCount = 0;
155	        this._playRechargeGlow(api.player.x, api.player.y, api.scene);
156	      }
157	      this._updateRevivalUI();
158	    }
159	
160	    if (this.isGoldForm) {
161	      this._strikeThunder(api, false);
162	      return;
163	    }
164	
165	    this.yeoijuCount++;
166	    this._updateCounter();
167	
168	    if (this.yeoijuCount >= MUGI_PARAMS.goldThreshold) {
169	      this._transformGold(api);
170	    } else {
171	      this._strikeRedLightning(api);
172	    }
173	  }
174	
175	  // ── 기운 (아우라) 이펙트 ─────────────────────────────────────────────
176	
177	  private _tickAura(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
178	    const level = this.isGoldForm ? 4 : Math.floor(this.yeoijuCount / 10);
179	    const delta = api.scene.game.loop.delta;
180	
181	    // 레벨별 검은/금색 기운
182	    if (level > 0) {
183	      this.auraTimer += delta;
184	      const interval = Math.max(70, 400 - level * 70);
185	      if (this.auraTimer >= interval) {
186	        this.auraTimer -= interval;
187	        const color = this.isGoldForm ? 0xffdd00 : 0x8800ff;
188	        for (let i = 0; i < level; i++) {
189	          this._spawnAuraWisp(api.player.x, api.player.y, color, level, api.scene);
190	        }
191	      }
192	    }
193	
194	    // 부활 충전 시 흰색 기운 (레벨 무관, 항상 일정 속도)
195	    if (this.revivalCharges > 0 && this.totalRevivalsUsed < 3) {
196	      this.revivalAuraTimer += delta;
197	      if (this.revivalAuraTimer >= 180) {
198	        this.revivalAuraTimer -= 180;
199	        this._spawnAuraWisp(api.player.x, api.player.y, 0xffffff, 3, api.scene);
200	        this._spawnAuraWisp(api.player.x, api.player.y, 0xffffff, 3, api.scene);
201	      }
202	    }
203	  }

205	  // glow_dot + ADD 블렌드로 여우불 질감 표현
206	  private _spawnAuraWisp(px: number, py: number, color: number, level: number, scene: Phaser.Scene): void {
207	    const angle  = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
208	    const spread = 18 + level * 4;
209	    const ox = px + Math.cos(angle) * Phaser.Math.Between(8, spread);
210	    const oy = py + Math.sin(angle) * Phaser.Math.Between(8, spread * 0.6);
211	    const sz  = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(3, 5 + level * 0.6);
212	    const glowScale = sz / 28;
213	
214	    // 외곽 글로우 (ADD 블렌드 — 뒤 화면색에 색을 더함)
215	    const img = scene.add.image(ox, oy, 'glow_dot')
216	      .setDepth(6)
217	      .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD)
218	      .setTint(color)
219	      .setScale(glowScale);
220	
221	    // 코어: 외곽 글로우색 → 중심 흰색으로 그라데이션 느낌
222	    const core = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(7).setPosition(ox, oy);
223	    core.fillStyle(color, 0.85);
224	    core.fillCircle(0, 0, Math.max(2, sz * 0.45));
225	    core.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.9);
226	    core.fillCircle(0, 0, Math.max(1, sz * 0.22));
227	
228	    const tx  = ox + Phaser.Math.Between(-14, 14);
229	    const ty  = oy - Phaser.Math.Between(22, 44);
230	    const dur = Phaser.Math.Between(480, 860);
231	
232	    scene.tweens.add({
233	      targets: img, x: tx, y: ty, alpha: 0, scale: glowScale * 0.3,
234	      duration: dur, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
235	      onComplete: () => { if (img.active) img.destroy(); },
236	    });
237	    scene.tweens.add({
238	      targets: core, x: tx, y: ty, alpha: 0, scale: 0.3,
239	      duration: dur, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
240	      onComplete: () => { if (core.active) core.destroy(); },
241	    });
242	  }
243	
244	  private _spawnCollectEffect(x: number, y: number, scene: Phaser.Scene): void {
245	    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
246	      const angle = (i / 6) * Math.PI * 2;
247	      const g = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(160);
248	      g.fillStyle(0xff3333, 1);
249	      g.fillCircle(0, 0, Phaser.Math.Between(2, 4));
250	      g.setPosition(x, y);
251	      scene.tweens.add({
252	        targets: g,
253	        x: x + Math.cos(angle) * Phaser.Math.Between(12, 28),
254	        y: y + Math.sin(angle) * Phaser.Math.Between(12, 28),
255	        alpha: 0, scale: 0,
256	        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(200, 360),
257	        ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
258	        onComplete: () => g.destroy(),
259	      });
260	    }
261	  }

266	  }
267	
268	  private _updateRevivalUI(): void {
269	    if (!this.revivalText) return;
270	    if (this.totalRevivalsUsed >= 3) {
271	      this.revivalText.setText('(부활 소진)').setColor('#666666');
272	    } else if (this.revivalCharges > 0) {
273	      this.revivalText.setText(`♡ 부활 (${3 - this.totalRevivalsUsed}회 남음)`).setColor('#ff66bb');
274	    } else {
275	      this.revivalText
276	        .setText(`○ 부활 충전 ${this.revivalChargeCount}/${MUGI_PARAMS.goldThreshold}`)
277	        .setColor('#aaaaaa');
278	    }
279	  }

281	  // ── 황금 변신 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
282	
283	  private _transformGold(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
284	    this.isGoldForm = true;
285	    this.yeoijus.forEach(orb => orb.gfx.destroy());
286	    this.yeoijus = [];
287	    this.counterText?.setVisible(false);
288	    this._strikeThunder(api, true);

291	  private _resetGoldForm(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
292	    this.isGoldForm = false;
293	    this.yeoijuCount = 0;
294	    api.player.setTexturePrefix(this.normalPrefix);
295	    this.counterText?.setVisible(true);
