```
298	
299	  // ── 황금 번개 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
300	
301	  // applyGoldSprite: true → 변신 시 (180ms 딜레이 후 gold 텍스처 적용), false → 이미 변신 상태
302	  private _strikeThunder(api: GameSceneAPI, applyGoldSprite: boolean): void {
303	    const { scene, player } = api;
304	    const tx = player.x;
305	    const ty = player.y;
306	
307	    scene.cameras.main.flash(600, 255, 230, 0, true);
308	
309	    const g = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(310);
310	
311	    const makePts = (startX: number) => {
312	      const pts: { x: number; y: number }[] = [{ x: startX, y: -10 }];
313	      for (let i = 1; i < 17; i++) {
314	        const t = i / 17;
315	        const spread = 70 * (1 - t * 0.65);
316	        pts.push({ x: tx + Phaser.Math.Between(-spread, spread), y: (ty + 20) * t - 10 });
317	      }
318	      pts.push({ x: tx, y: ty + 8 });
319	      return pts;
320	    };
321	
322	    const drawBolt = () => {
323	      g.clear();
324	      const pts = makePts(tx + Phaser.Math.Between(-25, 25));
325	      g.lineStyle(44, 0xffaa00, 0.09); this._drawPolyline(g, pts);
326	      g.lineStyle(22, 0xffcc00, 0.32); this._drawPolyline(g, pts);
327	      g.lineStyle(9,  0xffee44, 0.80); this._drawPolyline(g, pts);
328	      g.lineStyle(3,  0xffffff, 1.00); this._drawPolyline(g, pts);
329	      [3, 7, 11].forEach(bi => {
330	        if (bi >= pts.length - 1) return;
331	        const bp = pts[bi]!;
332	        const dir = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1;
333	        const len = Phaser.Math.Between(55, 105);
334	        const bPts = [
335	          { x: bp.x, y: bp.y },
336	          { x: bp.x + dir * len * 0.35 + Phaser.Math.Between(-18, 18), y: bp.y + len * 0.33 },
337	          { x: bp.x + dir * len * 0.68 + Phaser.Math.Between(-12, 12), y: bp.y + len * 0.65 },
338	          { x: bp.x + dir * len + Phaser.Math.Between(-8, 8),          y: bp.y + len },
339	        ];
340	        g.lineStyle(9,   0xffcc00, 0.40); this._drawPolyline(g, bPts);
341	        g.lineStyle(3,   0xffee44, 0.75); this._drawPolyline(g, bPts);
342	        g.lineStyle(1.5, 0xffffff, 0.90); this._drawPolyline(g, bPts);
343	      });
344	    };
345	
346	    drawBolt();
347	    [50, 100, 150, 200].forEach(delay => {
348	      scene.time.delayedCall(delay, () => {
349	        if (!scene.sys.isActive() || !g.active) return;
350	        drawBolt();
351	      });
352	    });
353	
354	    scene.tweens.add({
355	      targets: g, alpha: 0, delay: 240, duration: 380, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
356	      onComplete: () => g.destroy(),
357	    });
358	
359	    const impact = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(308);
360	    impact.fillStyle(0xffee00, 0.7);
361	    impact.fillCircle(tx, ty, 55);
362	    scene.tweens.add({
363	      targets: impact, scaleX: 2.8, scaleY: 2.8, alpha: 0,
364	      duration: 500, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
365	      onComplete: () => impact.destroy(),
366	    });
367	
368	    const ring = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(309);
369	    ring.lineStyle(4, 0xffffff, 1);
370	    ring.strokeCircle(tx, ty, 40);
371	    scene.tweens.add({
372	      targets: ring, scaleX: 3.5, scaleY: 3.5, alpha: 0,
373	      duration: 450, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
374	      onComplete: () => ring.destroy(),
375	    });
376	
377	    scene.time.delayedCall(180, () => {
378	      if (!scene.sys.isActive()) return;
379	      // 변신 도중 부활로 isGoldForm이 해제됐을 수 있으므로 확인
380	      if (applyGoldSprite && this.isGoldForm) {
381	        api.player.setTexturePrefix('gold_mugi_');
382	      }
383	      this._clearPoops(api, () => true, 20);
384	    });
385	  }
386	
387	  // ── 검붉은 번개 (변신 전) ────────────────────────────────────────────
388	
389	  private _strikeRedLightning(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
390	    const { scene, player } = api;
391	    const tx = player.x;
392	    const ty = player.y;
393	
394	    scene.cameras.main.flash(250, 120, 0, 0, true);
395	
396	    const g = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(310);
397	
398	    // _strikeThunder와 동일한 형태, 색만 검붉은 팔레트
399	    const makePts = (startX: number) => {
400	      const pts: { x: number; y: number }[] = [{ x: startX, y: -10 }];
401	      for (let i = 1; i < 17; i++) {
402	        const t = i / 17;
403	        const spread = 70 * (1 - t * 0.65);
404	        pts.push({ x: tx + Phaser.Math.Between(-spread, spread), y: (ty + 20) * t - 10 });
405	      }
406	      pts.push({ x: tx, y: ty + 8 });
407	      return pts;
408	    };
409	
410	    const drawRed = () => {
411	      g.clear();
412	      const pts = makePts(tx + Phaser.Math.Between(-25, 25));
413	      g.lineStyle(30, 0x440000, 0.09); this._drawPolyline(g, pts);
414	      g.lineStyle(14, 0x880000, 0.32); this._drawPolyline(g, pts);
415	      g.lineStyle(6,  0x080000, 0.92); this._drawPolyline(g, pts); // 코어 바로 외곽 — 진한 검정
416	      g.lineStyle(2,  0xff3311, 1.00); this._drawPolyline(g, pts);
417	      [3, 7, 11].forEach(bi => {
418	        if (bi >= pts.length - 1) return;
419	        const bp = pts[bi]!;
420	        const dir = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1;
421	        const len = Phaser.Math.Between(55, 105);
422	        const bPts = [
423	          { x: bp.x, y: bp.y },
424	          { x: bp.x + dir * len * 0.35 + Phaser.Math.Between(-18, 18), y: bp.y + len * 0.33 },
425	          { x: bp.x + dir * len * 0.68 + Phaser.Math.Between(-12, 12), y: bp.y + len * 0.65 },
426	          { x: bp.x + dir * len + Phaser.Math.Between(-8, 8),          y: bp.y + len },
427	        ];
428	        g.lineStyle(6,   0x880000, 0.40); this._drawPolyline(g, bPts);
429	        g.lineStyle(2,   0x080000, 0.88); this._drawPolyline(g, bPts);
430	        g.lineStyle(1,   0xff3311, 0.90); this._drawPolyline(g, bPts);
431	      });
432	    };
433	
434	    drawRed();
435	    [55, 110, 165].forEach(delay => {
436	      scene.time.delayedCall(delay, () => {
437	        if (!scene.sys.isActive() || !g.active) return;
438	        drawRed();
439	      });
440	    });
441	
442	    scene.tweens.add({
443	      targets: g, alpha: 0, delay: 210, duration: 300, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
444	      onComplete: () => g.destroy(),
445	    });
446	
447	    const impact = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(308);
448	    impact.fillStyle(0xaa0000, 0.55);
449	    impact.fillCircle(tx, ty, 35);
450	    scene.tweens.add({
451	      targets: impact, scaleX: 2.2, scaleY: 2.2, alpha: 0,
452	      duration: 380, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
453	      onComplete: () => impact.destroy(),
454	    });
455	
456	    scene.time.delayedCall(180, () => {
457	      if (!scene.sys.isActive()) return;
458	      this._clearPoops(api, p => Math.abs(p.x - tx) <= 60, 10);
459	    });
460	  }
461	
462	  // ── 공통 유틸 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
463	
464	  private _clearPoops(
465	    api: GameSceneAPI,
466	    filter: (p: Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite) => boolean,
467	    pointsPerPoop: number,
468	  ): void {
469	    const { poops, scene } = api;
470	    const targets = (poops.getChildren() as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite[])
471	      .filter(p => p.active && filter(p));
472	
473	    if (targets.length === 0) return;
474	
475	    // 위치를 먼저 저장한 뒤 즉시 재활용 — 피버 타임이 얼어있는 똥을
476	    // 특수 똥으로 변환하는 race condition을 원천 차단
477	    const positions = targets.map(p => ({ x: p.x, y: p.y }));
478	    targets.forEach(p => (p as unknown as PoolablePoopBase).recycle());
479	
480	    // 잽 이펙트는 저장된 위치에서 독립적으로 재생
481	    positions.forEach(({ x, y }) => this._spawnZapEffect(x, y, scene));
482	
483	    scene.time.delayedCall(450, () => {
484	      if (!scene.sys.isActive()) return;
485	      api.addAbilityBonus(positions.length * pointsPerPoop);
486	    });
487	  }
488	
489	  private _drawPolyline(g: Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics, pts: { x: number; y: number }[]): void {
490	    g.beginPath();
491	    g.moveTo(pts[0]!.x, pts[0]!.y);
492	    for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) g.lineTo(pts[i]!.x, pts[i]!.y);
493	    g.strokePath();
494	  }
495	
496	  private _spawnZapEffect(x: number, y: number, scene: Phaser.Scene): void {
497	    const g = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(206);
498	
499	    const draw = () => {
500	      g.clear();
501	      const sparks = Phaser.Math.Between(3, 5);
502	      for (let i = 0; i < sparks; i++) {
503	        const color = Math.random() < 0.55 ? 0xffee00 : 0xffffff;
504	        g.lineStyle(Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(1.5, 2.5), color, 1);
505	        g.beginPath();
506	        let px = x + Phaser.Math.Between(-7, 7);
507	        let py = y + Phaser.Math.Between(-7, 7);
508	        g.moveTo(px, py);
509	        for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++) {
510	          px += Phaser.Math.Between(-10, 10);
511	          py += Phaser.Math.Between(-10, 10);
512	          g.lineTo(px, py);
513	        }
514	        g.strokePath();
515	      }
516	    };
517	
518	    draw();
519	    const timer = scene.time.addEvent({ delay: 80, repeat: 5, callback: draw });
520	    scene.time.delayedCall(550, () => {
521	      timer.remove();
522	      if (g.active) g.destroy();
523	    });
524	  }
525	
526	  // ── 부활 시스템 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
527	
528	  private _revive(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
529	    this.revivalCharges--;
530	    this.totalRevivalsUsed++;
531	
532	    if (this.isGoldForm) {
533	      this._resetGoldForm(api);
534	    }
535	    this._updateRevivalUI();
536	
537	    const { poops, scene } = api;
538	
539	    // 전체 똥 즉시 제거
540	    (poops.getChildren() as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite[])
541	      .filter(p => p.active)
542	      .forEach(p => (p as unknown as PoolablePoopBase).recycle());
543	
544	    // 화면 중앙으로 이동 + 연꽃 소환
545	    const centerX = scene.scale.width / 2;
546	    api.player.setX(centerX);
547	
548	    scene.cameras.main.flash(350, 255, 220, 240, true);
549	    api.player.setInvincibleBriefly(2500);
550	    this._playRevivalLotus(centerX, api.player.y, scene);
551	  }
552	
553	  private _playRevivalLotus(px: number, py: number, scene: Phaser.Scene): void {
554	    const LS = 2.2;                // 연꽃 전체 스케일
555	    const cx = px;
556	    const cy = py + 38;            // 플레이어 발 바닥 (displayH=80 → 중심 +40, base offset -2)
557	
558	    // 꽃받침
559	    const base = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(352).setScale(0);
560	    base.fillStyle(0x66bb6a, 0.85); base.fillEllipse(0, 0, 28 * LS, 9 * LS);
561	    base.fillStyle(0x81c784, 0.6);  base.fillEllipse(0, -2 * LS, 16 * LS, 5 * LS);
562	    base.setPosition(cx, cy + 2);
563	    scene.tweens.add({ targets: base, scale: 1, duration: 220, ease: 'Back.easeOut' });
564	
565	    // 금빛 중앙 글로우
566	    const glow = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(353).setScale(0);
567	    glow.fillStyle(0xffd700, 0.20); glow.fillCircle(0, 0, 30 * LS);
568	    glow.fillStyle(0xffe082, 0.35); glow.fillCircle(0, 0, 18 * LS);
569	    glow.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.55); glow.fillCircle(0, 0,  8 * LS);
570	    glow.setPosition(cx, cy - 14 * LS);
571	    scene.tweens.add({ targets: glow, scale: 1, duration: 320, ease: 'Back.easeOut' });
572	
573	    // sin(t*π) 곡선으로 양 끝 뾰족한 꽃잎 폴리곤
574	    const petalPts = (w: number, h: number): { x: number; y: number }[] => {
575	      const N = 16;
576	      const pts: { x: number; y: number }[] = [];
577	      for (let i = 0; i <= N; i++) {
578	        const t = i / N;
579	        pts.push({ x:  (w / 2) * Math.sin(t * Math.PI), y: h / 2 - t * h });
580	      }
581	      for (let i = N - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
582	        const t = i / N;
583	        pts.push({ x: -(w / 2) * Math.sin(t * Math.PI), y: h / 2 - t * h });
584	      }
585	      return pts;
586	    };
587	
588	    // 꽃잎: 바닥 한 점에서 각도 방향으로 뻗어나가는 옆면 형태
589	    const petalGfxs: Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics[] = [];
590	    LOTUS_DEFS.forEach(([rotDeg, w, h, color, depthOff], idx) => {
591	      const sw = w * LS;
592	      const sh = h * LS;
593	      const rotRad = rotDeg * Math.PI / 180;
594	      const ppx = cx + Math.sin(rotRad) * (sh / 2);
595	      const ppy = (cy + 2) - Math.cos(rotRad) * (sh / 2);
596	
597	      const g = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(353 + depthOff);
598	      g.fillStyle(color, 0.92);
599	      g.fillPoints(petalPts(sw, sh), true);
600	      g.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.45);
601	      g.fillPoints(petalPts(sw * 0.35, sh * 0.65), true);
602	      g.fillStyle(0xffd700, 0.18);
603	      g.fillEllipse(0, -(sh * 0.3), sw * 0.55, sh * 0.22);
604	      g.setPosition(ppx, ppy).setRotation(rotRad).setScale(0);
605	
606	      scene.tweens.add({ targets: g, scale: 1, delay: idx * 40, duration: 280, ease: 'Back.easeOut' });
607	      petalGfxs.push(g);
608	    });
609	
610	    // 수술
611	    const stamen = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(359).setScale(0);
612	    stamen.fillStyle(0xffd700, 1); stamen.fillCircle(0, 0, 5 * LS);
613	    stamen.fillStyle(0xffff99, 0.9); stamen.fillCircle(0, 0, 3 * LS);
614	    stamen.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.7); stamen.fillCircle(-1 * LS, -1.5 * LS, 1.5 * LS);
615	    stamen.setPosition(cx, cy - 20 * LS);
616	    scene.tweens.add({
617	      targets: stamen, scale: 1,
618	      delay: LOTUS_DEFS.length * 40 - 20, duration: 220, ease: 'Back.easeOut',
619	    });
620	
621	    // 900ms 후 조용히 페이드아웃
622	    const allObjs: Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics[] = [...petalGfxs, glow, base, stamen];
623	    scene.time.delayedCall(900, () => {
624	      if (!scene.sys.isActive()) return;
625	      allObjs.forEach(obj => {
626	        scene.tweens.add({
627	          targets: obj, alpha: 0,
628	          duration: 500, ease: 'Quad.easeIn',
629	          onComplete: () => { if (obj.active) obj.destroy(); },
630	        });
631	      });
632	    });
633	  }
634	
635	  private _playRechargeGlow(px: number, py: number, scene: Phaser.Scene): void {
636	    const g = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(320);
637	    g.lineStyle(4, 0xff88cc, 1);
638	    g.strokeCircle(px, py, 30);
639	    scene.tweens.add({
640	      targets: g, scaleX: 2.5, scaleY: 2.5, alpha: 0,
641	      duration: 500, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
642	      onComplete: () => g.destroy(),
643	    });
644	  }
645	
646	  // ── 정리 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
647	
648	  override onDestroy(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
649	    this.yeoijus.forEach(orb => orb.gfx.destroy());
650	    this.yeoijus = [];
651	    this.counterText?.destroy();
652	    this.counterText = null;
653	    this.revivalText?.destroy();
654	    this.revivalText = null;
655	    if (this.isGoldForm) {
656	      api.player.setTexturePrefix(this.normalPrefix);
657	      this.isGoldForm = false;
658	    }
659	  }
660	}
661	
```

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
`_spawnAuraWisp`는 매 wisp마다 Graphics를 생성하는데, 황금 형태에서는 120ms마다 4개씩 즉 초당 33개 객체 생성/파괴가 반복됩니다. `glow_dot` Image로 교체하면 WebGL draw call 방식이 Graphics(개별 GPU 패스)에서 Image(스프라이트 배치)로 변경되어 GPU 부하가 급감합니다.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/johyunduk/Projects/ddong-avoid-game/src/abilities/LegacyAbility.ts",
  "content": "import { BaseAbility } from './BaseAbility';\nimport type { GameSceneAPI } from './types';\nimport type PoolablePoopBase from '../objects/PoolablePoopBase';\nimport { LEGACY_PARAMS } from '../config/abilityParams';\nimport { ensureGlowDot } from '../utils/glowDot';\n\n/**\n * 레거시 (UR) — 피버 + 레거시 모드\n * ★0: 6초 피버 / 불태우기 30%/2개(일반) 60%/4개(레거시) / 500점마다 / 10초 / 1.2배\n * ★1: 8초 / 35%/2개 65%/4개 / 450점마다 / 10초 / 1.25배\n * ★2: 10초 / 40%/3개 70%/4개 / 400점마다 / 12초 / 1.30배\n * ★3: 12초 / 45%/3개 75%/4개 / 350점마다 / 15초 / 1.35배 + 모드 종료 직후 3초 미니 피버\n */\n\ninterface RainStarData {\n  startX: number; startY: number;\n  travelX: number; travelY: number;\n  startRot: number; rotDelta: number;\n  alpha: number; whiteAlpha: number;\n  s: number; col: number;\n  startTime: number; endTime: number; duration: number;\n}\n\nexport class LegacyAbility extends BaseAbility {\n  private startFeverActive = false;\n  private legacyModeActive = false;\n  private accum           = 0;\n  private lastLegacyScore = 0;\n\n  private legacyTopGlow?:       Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics;\n  private legacyPulseTween?:    Phaser.Tweens.Tween;\n  private legacyRainTimer?:     Phaser.Time.TimerEvent;\n  private legacyEndTimer?:      Phaser.Time.TimerEvent;\n  private startFeverTimer?:     Phaser.Time.TimerEvent;\n  private startFeverEndTimer?:  Phaser.Time.TimerEvent;\n\n  // A: 상시 불꽃 오라\n  private oraTimer = 0;\n  private static readonly ORA_INTERVAL_NORMAL = 180;\n  private static readonly ORA_INTERVAL_LEGACY =  80;\n  private static readonly ORA_COLS_NORMAL = [0xff3300, 0xff6600, 0xffaa00];\n  private static readonly ORA_COLS_LEGACY = [0xffaa00, 0xffcc00, 0xffee44, 0xffffff];\n\n  // D: 빗줄기 색상\n  private static readonly RAIN_COLS_FEVER  = [0xff8800, 0xffaa00, 0xffcc00, 0xffee00];\n  private static readonly RAIN_COLS_LEGACY = [0xff5500, 0xff8800, 0xffaa00, 0xffcc00];\n\n  // D: 빗줄기 별 배치 — 단일 Graphics + 데이터 배열로 N개 draw call → 1개로 축소\n  private rainStarGfx: Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics | null = null;\n  private rainStars: RainStarData[] = [];\n\n  // 8각 별 정규화 좌표 (크기=1) — 프레임당 재사용하는 회전 버퍼와 함께 GC 압력 제거\n  private static readonly _STAR_NORM: { x: number; y: number }[] = [\n    { x:  0,    y: -1    }, { x:  0.4, y: -0.4 },\n    { x:  1,    y:  0    }, { x:  0.4, y:  0.4 },\n    { x:  0,    y:  1    }, { x: -0.4, y:  0.4 },\n    { x: -1,    y:  0    }, { x: -0.4, y: -0.4 },\n  ];\n  private static readonly _STAR_BUF: { x: number; y: number }[] =\n    Array.from({ length: 8 }, () => ({ x: 0, y: 0 }));\n\n  // ── 불꽃 물방울 다각형 좌표 ─────────────────────────────────────────\n  private static flamePts(w: number, h: number) {\n    return [\n      { x:  0,         y: -h        },\n      { x:  w * 0.55,  y: -h * 0.1  },\n      { x:  w * 0.75,  y:  h * 0.35 },\n      { x:  w * 0.3,   y:  h        },\n      { x: -w * 0.3,   y:  h        },\n      { x: -w * 0.75,  y:  h * 0.35 },\n      { x: -w * 0.55,  y: -h * 0.1  },\n    ];\n  }\n\n  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  // 생명주기\n  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  override onCreate(api: GameSceneAPI): void {\n    ensureGlowDot(api.scene);\n    this.rainStarGfx = api.scene.add.graphics().setDepth(85);\n    this.rainStars   = [];\n\n    this.startFeverActive = true;\n    this.startFeverRain(api);\n    this.startFeverEndTimer = api.scene.time.delayedCall(LEGACY_PARAMS.feverDuration, () => {\n      this.startFeverActive = false;\n    });\n  }\n\n  override onUpdate(api: GameSceneAPI): void {\n    const delta = api.scene.game.loop.delta;\n    this.oraTimer += delta;\n    const interval = this.legacyModeActive\n      ? LegacyAbility.ORA_INTERVAL_LEGACY\n      : LegacyAbility.ORA_INTERVAL_NORMAL;\n    if (this.oraTimer >= interval) {\n      this.oraTimer -= interval;\n      this.spawnOraFlame(api);\n    }\n\n    if (this.rainStars.length > 0) {\n      this._updateRainStars(api.scene);\n    }\n  }\n\n  override getTickScore(base: number): number {\n    if (!this.legacyModeActive) return base;\n    this.accum += base * LEGACY_PARAMS.scoreExtra;\n    const bonus = Math.floor(this.accum);\n    this.accum -= bonus;\n    return base + bonus;\n  }\n\n  override overrideSpawnPoop(api: GameSceneAPI): boolean {\n    if (this.startFeverActive) {\n      api.spawnGoldPoop();\n      api.spawnGoldPoop();\n      return true;\n    }\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  override onAfterSpawnPoop(api: GameSceneAPI): void {\n    const chance = this.legacyModeActive ? LEGACY_PARAMS.burnChanceLegacy : LEGACY_PARAMS.burnChanceNormal;\n    const count  = this.legacyModeActive ? LEGACY_PARAMS.burnCountLegacy  : LEGACY_PARAMS.burnCountNormal;\n    if (Math.random() < chance) {\n      this.burnRandomPoops(api, count);\n    }\n  }\n\n  override onScoreMilestone(score: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {\n    if (score % LEGACY_PARAMS.legacyInterval === 0 && score > this.lastLegacyScore) {\n      this.lastLegacyScore = score;\n      this.activateLegacyMode(api);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  // A: 상시 불꽃 오라 — glow_dot Image (Graphics → Image, 드로우콜 제거)\n  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  private spawnOraFlame(api: GameSceneAPI): void {\n    const scene = api.scene;\n    const px    = api.player.x;\n    const py    = api.player.y;\n    const leg   = this.legacyModeActive;\n    const cols  = leg ? LegacyAbility.ORA_COLS_LEGACY : LegacyAbility.ORA_COLS_NORMAL;\n    const count = leg ? Phaser.Math.Between(5, 7) : Phaser.Math.Between(2, 3);\n    const rMax  = leg ? 2.2  : 1.6;\n    const aMax  = leg ? 0.90 : 0.70;\n    const riseMin = leg ? 20  : 14;\n    const riseMax = leg ? 42  : 28;\n    const lifeMin = leg ? 280 : 180;\n    const lifeMax = leg ? 520 : 380;\n\n    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n      const r   = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0.8, rMax);\n      const col = Phaser.Utils.Array.GetRandom(cols) as number;\n      const a   = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0.50, aMax);\n      const x   = px + Phaser.Math.Between(-10, 10);\n      const y   = py + Phaser.Math.Between(10, 24);\n\n      const img = scene.add.image(x, y, 'glow_dot')\n        .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD)\n        .setTint(col)\n        .setScale(r / 10)\n        .setAlpha(a)\n        .setDepth(88);\n\n      scene.tweens.add({\n        targets:  img,\n        y:        y - Phaser.Math.Between(riseMin, riseMax),\n        x:        x + Phaser.Math.Between(-4, 4),\n        alpha:    0,\n        scale:    0,\n        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(lifeMin, lifeMax),\n        ease:     'Quad.easeOut',\n        onComplete: () => { if (img.active) img.destroy(); },\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  // B: 화염 추적선\n  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  private drawFireTrail(\n    scene: Phaser.Scene,\n    fromX: number, fromY: number,\n    toList: { x: number; y: number }[],\n  ): void {\n    if (toList.length === 0) return;\n\n    const gfx = scene.add.graphics();\n    gfx.setDepth(195);\n\n    for (const { x, y } of toList) {\n      gfx.lineStyle(3.5, 0xff6600, 0.50);\n      gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(fromX, fromY); gfx.lineTo(x, y); gfx.strokePath();\n      gfx.lineStyle(1.5, 0xffee00, 0.75);\n      gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(fromX, fromY); gfx.lineTo(x, y); gfx.strokePath();\n    }\n\n    scene.tweens.add({\n      targets: gfx, alpha: 0, duration: 200, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',\n      onComplete: () => { gfx.destroy(); },\n    });\n  }\n\n  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  // C: 레거시 모드 활성화\n  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  private activateLegacyMode(api: GameSceneAPI): void {\n    if (this.legacyModeActive) return;\n    this.legacyModeActive = true;\n    this.accum = 0;\n\n    const scene = api.scene;\n    const cx    = api.player.x;\n    const cy    = api.player.y;\n\n    // ─ 반투명 플래시 ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n    const flashOverlay = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(200).setScrollFactor(0);\n    flashOverlay.fillStyle(0xffee00, 1);\n    flashOverlay.fillRect(0, 0, scene.scale.width, scene.scale.height);\n    flashOverlay.setAlpha(0.38);\n    scene.tweens.add({\n      targets: flashOverlay, alpha: 0, duration: 220, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',\n      onComplete: () => { flashOverlay.destroy(); },\n    });\n\n    // ─ 팽창 링 2개 ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n    [{ delay: 0, maxScale: 4.3 }, { delay: 80, maxScale: 3.4 }].forEach(({ delay, maxScale }) => {\n      const ring = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(155);\n      ring.lineStyle(2.5, 0xffee00, 0.9);\n      ring.strokeEllipse(0, 0, 74, 98);\n      ring.setPosition(cx, cy);\n      scene.tweens.add({\n        targets: ring, scaleX: maxScale, scaleY: maxScale, alpha: 0,\n        duration: 500, delay, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',\n        onComplete: () => { ring.destroy(); },\n      });\n    });\n\n    // ─ 채움 타원 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    const fill = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(154);\n    fill.fillStyle(0xffaa00, 0.28);\n    fill.fillEllipse(0, 0, 74, 98);\n    fill.setPosition(cx, cy);\n    scene.tweens.add({\n      targets: fill, alpha: 0, duration: 180, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',\n      onComplete: () => { fill.destroy(); },\n    });\n\n    // ─ 파편 8개 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {\n      const angle = (i / 8) * Math.PI * 2 + Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(-0.25, 0.25);\n      const speed = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(40, 95);\n      const sw    = Phaser.Math.Between(5, 12);\n      const sh    = Phaser.Math.Between(2, 5);\n      const shard = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(156);\n      shard.fillStyle(0xffee00, 1);\n      shard.fillRect(-sw / 2, -sh / 2, sw, sh);\n      shard.setPosition(cx, cy).setRotation(angle);\n      scene.tweens.add({\n        targets: shard,\n        x: cx + Math.cos(angle) * speed, y: cy + Math.sin(angle) * speed,\n        alpha: 0, rotation: angle + Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(-1, 1),\n        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(270, 440), ease: 'Quad.easeOut',\n        onComplete: () => { shard.destroy(); },\n      });\n    }\n\n    // ─ 화면 전체 불꽃 폭발 ────────────────────────────────\n    const BURST_COLS = [0xff2200, 0xff6600, 0xffaa00, 0xffdd00, 0xffff88];\n    for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {\n      const gfx = scene.add.graphics();\n      const w   = Phaser.Math.Between(4, 10);\n      const h   = Phaser.Math.Between(10, 22);\n      const col = Phaser.Utils.Array.GetRandom(BURST_COLS) as number;\n\n      gfx.fillStyle(col, Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0.22, 0.50));\n      gfx.fillPoints(LegacyAbility.flamePts(w, h), true);\n      gfx.fillStyle(0xffee88, Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0.28, 0.55));\n      gfx.fillPoints(LegacyAbility.flamePts(w * 0.4, h * 0.5), true);\n\n      gfx.setPosition(Phaser.Math.Between(20, scene.scale.width - 20), Phaser.Math.Between(40, scene.scale.height - 40));\n      gfx.setRotation(Phaser.Math.DegToRad(Phaser.Math.Between(-180, 180)));\n      gfx.setDepth(158).setAlpha(0);\n\n      scene.tweens.add({\n        targets: gfx, alpha: 1,\n        scaleX: Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(1.2, 1.9),\n        scaleY: Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(1.2, 1.9),\n        duration: 140, delay: Phaser.Math.Between(0, 280), ease: 'Quad.easeOut',\n        onComplete: () => {\n          scene.tweens.add({\n            targets: gfx, alpha: 0, y: gfx.y - Phaser.Math.Between(20, 50),\n            duration: Phaser.Math.Between(380, 650), ease: 'Quad.easeIn',\n            onComplete: () => { gfx.destroy(); },\n          });\n        },\n      });\n    }\n\n    // ─ 상단 노란빛 그라데이션 ────────────────────────────────────\n    this.legacyTopGlow = scene.add.graphics();\n    this.legacyTopGlow.fillGradientStyle(0xffee00, 0xffee00, 0xffee00, 0xffee00, 0.28, 0.28, 0, 0);\n    this.legacyTopGlow.fillRect(0, 0, scene.scale.width, Math.round(scene.scale.height * 0.37));\n    this.legacyTopGlow.setDepth(148).setScrollFactor(0);\n\n    this.legacyPulseTween = scene.tweens.add({\n      targets: [this.legacyTopGlow], alpha: 0.55,\n      duration: 600, yoyo: true, repeat: -1, ease: 'Sine.easeInOut',\n    });\n\n    // ─ 레거시 모드 황금 빗줄기 (repeat: -1, 10초 후 remove로 정리) ─\n    this.legacyRainTimer = scene.time.addEvent({\n      delay: 120,\n      repeat: -1,\n      callback: () => { this.spawnRainStar(scene, LegacyAbility.RAIN_COLS_LEGACY, 0.75, 1.00, 3); },\n    });\n\n    // 레거시 모드 종료\n    this.legacyEndTimer = scene.time.delayedCall(LEGACY_PARAMS.legacyDuration, () => {\n      this.legacyModeActive = false;\n      this.legacyRainTimer?.remove();\n      this.legacyRainTimer = undefined;\n      this.legacyPulseTween?.stop();\n      this.legacyPulseTween = undefined;\n      scene.tweens.add({\n        targets: [this.legacyTopGlow], alpha: 0, duration: 400, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',\n        onComplete: () => {\n          this.legacyTopGlow?.destroy();\n          this.legacyTopGlow = undefined;\n        },\n      });\n\n    });\n  }\n\n  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  // D: 황금 빗줄기 — 단일 Graphics 배치 방식\n  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  private startFeverRain(api: GameSceneAPI): void {\n    const scene  = api.scene;\n    const REPEAT = Math.floor(LEGACY_PARAMS.feverDuration / 140) - 1;\n\n    this.startFeverTimer = scene.time.addEvent({\n      delay: 140,\n      repeat: REPEAT,\n      callback: () => { this.spawnRainStar(scene, LegacyAbility.RAIN_COLS_FEVER, 0.60, 0.90, 2); },\n    });\n  }\n\n  /** 빗줄기 별 데이터를 배열에 추가 — 실제 렌더링은 _updateRainStars에서 단일 Graphics로 일괄 처리 */\n  private spawnRainStar(\n    scene: Phaser.Scene,\n    cols: readonly number[],\n    aMin: number,\n    aMax: number,\n    count: number,\n  ): void {\n    if (!this.rainStarGfx) return;\n    const now = scene.time.now;\n    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n      const s        = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(2.5, 5.5);\n      const col      = Phaser.Utils.Array.GetRandom(cols as number[]) as number;\n      const alpha    = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(aMin, aMax);\n      const duration = Phaser.Math.Between(2200, 3600);\n      this.rainStars.push({\n        startX:     Phaser.Math.Between(10, scene.scale.width - 10),\n        startY:     -15,\n        travelX:    Phaser.Math.Between(-25, 25),\n        travelY:    Phaser.Math.Between(450, 600),\n        startRot:   Phaser.Math.DegToRad(Phaser.Math.Between(0, 360)),\n        rotDelta:   Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(1.5, 3.0),\n        alpha,\n        whiteAlpha: aMax > 0.85 ? 0.65 : 0.50,\n        s, col,\n        startTime: now,\n        endTime:   now + duration,\n        duration,\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 단일 Graphics를 clear → 전체 별 재드로우 (N draw call → 1) */\n  private _updateRainStars(scene: Phaser.Scene): void {\n    const gfx = this.rainStarGfx;\n    if (!gfx) return;\n    const now = scene.time.now;\n    gfx.clear();\n    const remaining: RainStarData[] = [];\n    for (const star of this.rainStars) {\n      if (now >= star.endTime) continue;\n      const t   = (now - star.startTime) / star.duration;\n      const cx  = star.startX + t * star.travelX;\n      const cy  = star.startY + t * star.travelY;\n      const rot = star.startRot + t * star.rotDelta;\n      const a   = star.alpha * (1 - t);\n      const wA  = star.whiteAlpha * (1 - t);\n\n      gfx.fillStyle(star.col, a);\n      gfx.fillPoints(this._rotatedStarPts(cx, cy, star.s, rot), true);\n      gfx.fillStyle(0xffffff, wA);\n      gfx.fillCircle(cx, cy, star.s * 0.32);\n\n      remaining.push(star);\n    }\n    this.rainStars = remaining;\n  }\n\n  /** 8각 별 좌표를 회전·스케일·이동 적용해 정적 버퍼에 in-place 기록 (GC 제로) */\n  private _rotatedStarPts(cx: number, cy: number, s: number, rot: number): { x: number; y: number }[] {\n    const buf  = LegacyAbility._STAR_BUF;\n    const norm = LegacyAbility._STAR_NORM;\n    const cos  = Math.cos(rot);\n    const sin  = Math.sin(rot);\n    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {\n      const p  = norm[i]!;\n      buf[i]!.x = cx + (p.x * cos - p.y * sin) * s;\n      buf[i]!.y = cy + (p.x * sin + p.y * cos) * s;\n    }\n    return buf;\n  }\n\n  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  // 불태우기 로직\n  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  private burnRandomPoops(api: GameSceneAPI, count: number): void {\n    const visible = api.poops.getChildren().filter(\n      c => c.active && (c as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite).y > -10,\n    ) as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite[];\n\n    if (visible.length === 0) return;\n\n    // Partial Fisher-Yates: count개만 앞으로 뽑아내고 나머지 순회 생략\n    const pool = visible.slice();\n    const take = Math.min(count, pool.length);\n    for (let i = 0; i < take; i++) {\n      const j = i + Math.floor(Math.random() * (pool.length - i));\n      [pool[i], pool[j]] = [pool[j], pool[i]];\n    }\n    const targets = pool.slice(0, take);\n\n    this.drawFireTrail(\n      api.scene,\n      api.player.x, api.player.y,\n      targets.map(p => ({ x: p.x, y: p.y })),\n    );\n\n    for (const poop of targets) {\n      if (!poop.active) continue;\n      this.playBurnEffect(api.scene, poop.x, poop.y);\n      (poop as PoolablePoopBase).recycle();\n      api.addAbilityBonus(10);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  // 리소스 정리 (게임 오버 시 GameScene에서 호출)\n  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  override onDestroy(_api: GameSceneAPI): void {\n    this.startFeverEndTimer?.remove();\n    this.startFeverTimer?.remove();\n    this.legacyEndTimer?.remove();\n    this.legacyRainTimer?.remove();\n    this.legacyPulseTween?.stop();\n    this.legacyTopGlow?.destroy();\n    this.rainStarGfx?.destroy();\n    this.rainStarGfx = null;\n    this.rainStars = [];\n  }\n\n  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  // 불꽃 소멸 이펙트\n  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  private playBurnEffect(scene: Phaser.Scene, cx: number, cy: number): void {\n    const flash = scene.add.graphics();\n    flash.fillStyle(0xff7700, 0.60); flash.fillCircle(0, 0, 17);\n    flash.fillStyle(0xffee88, 0.45); flash.fillCircle(0, 0, 9);\n    flash.setPosition(cx, cy).setDepth(202);\n    scene.tweens.add({\n      targets: flash, scaleX: 2.8, scaleY: 2.8, alpha: 0,\n      duration: 190, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',\n      onComplete: () => { flash.destroy(); },\n    });\n\n    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {\n      const gfx = scene.add.graphics();\n      const w   = Phaser.Math.Between(5, 11);\n      const h   = Phaser.Math.Between(14, 27);\n\n      gfx.fillStyle(0xaa1100, 0.25); gfx.fillPoints(LegacyAbility.flamePts(w,        h       ), true);\n      gfx.fillStyle(0xff4400, 0.48); gfx.fillPoints(LegacyAbility.flamePts(w * 0.70, h * 0.76), true);\n      gfx.fillStyle(0xffcc00, 0.68); gfx.fillPoints(LegacyAbility.flamePts(w * 0.40, h * 0.50), true);\n\n      gfx.setPosition(\n        cx + Phaser.Math.Between(-15, 15),\n        cy + Phaser.Math.Between(-10, 10),\n      );\n      gfx.setRotation(Phaser.Math.DegToRad(Phaser.Math.Between(-28, 28)));\n      gfx.setDepth(200);\n\n      const rise = Phaser.Math.Between(48, 88);\n      const dur  = Phaser.Math.Between(500, 900);\n\n      scene.tweens.add({\n        targets: gfx, y: gfx.y - rise * 0.38, scaleX: 1.45, scaleY: 1.45,\n        duration: dur * 0.35, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',\n        onComplete: () => {\n          scene.tweens.add({\n            targets: gfx, y: gfx.y - rise * 0.62, scaleX: 0, scaleY: 0, alpha: 0,\n            duration: dur * 0.65, ease: 'Quad.easeIn',\n            onComplete: () => { gfx.destroy(); 타입 에러 없이 통과했습니다.
```

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**LegacyAbility 빗줄기 최적화의 핵심 수치:**
- 기존: 120ms마다 3개 Graphics 생성, 각 2200~3600ms 생존 → 정상 상태에서 **최대 90개 Graphics 동시 존재**
- 변경 후: 단일 `rainStarGfx`가 `clear()` + 전체 재드로우 → **90개 draw call → 1개**

**`_STAR_BUF` 정적 버퍼 패턴:** 프레임마다 8개 별 꼭짓점을 계산할 때 `new { x, y }` 객체 대신 클래스 레벨 정적 배열을 in-place 덮어씁니다. 단일 스레드 환경에서는 다음 별 계산 전에 `fillPoints`가 완료되므로 경쟁 조건이 없습니다. `KnightAbility`의 `_bufs` 패턴과 동일한 원리입니다.

**MugiAbility:** Graphics → Image 교체로 wisp 1개당 2개 draw call이 1개로 줄었습니다. 황금 형태에서는 초당 약 **33개 → 16개** draw call 절감 효과입니다.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

## 최적화 완료 요약

이번 세션 전체에서 수행한 퍼포먼스 개선 목록:

| 파일 | 문제 | 수정 |
|------|------|------|
| `GameScene.ts` | `checkFeverTime` O(k) while 루프 | O(1) 캐시 (`nextFeverScore`/`feverScoreK`) |
| `GameScene.ts` | `spawnMaehwaStorm` 55개 Graphics | 단일 Graphics clear+redraw |
| `GameScene.ts` | `updateFeverTime` 매 100ms 텍스트 갱신 | 초가 바뀔 때만 갱신 (1/초) |
| `GachaScene.ts` | 슬라이드쇼 grade 이미지 destroy+create 반복 | `setTexture` 재사용 |
| `MugiAbility.ts` | `_spawnAuraWisp` Graphics 코어 | `glow_dot` Image로 교체 |
| `LegacyAbility.ts` | `spawnOraFlame` Graphics 80ms마다 생성 | `glow_dot` Image로 교체 |
| `LegacyAbility.ts` | `spawnRainStar` 최대 90개 Graphics 동시 존재 | 단일 Graphics + `RainStarData[]` 배열 관리 |

```
+  /** 단일 Graphics를 clear → 전체 별 재드로우 (N draw call → 1) */
+  private _updateRainStars(scene: Phaser.Scene): void {
+    const gfx = this.rainStarGfx;
+    if (!gfx) return;
+    const now = scene.time.now;
+    gfx.clear();
+    const remaining: RainStarData[] = [];
+    for (const star of this.rainStars) {
+      if (now >= star.endTime) continue;
+      const t   = (now - star.startTime) / star.duration;
+      const cx  = star.startX + t * star.travelX;
+      const cy  = star.startY + t * star.travelY;
+      const rot = star.startRot + t * star.rotDelta;
+      const a   = star.alpha * (1 - t);
+      const wA  = star.whiteAlpha * (1 - t);
+
+      gfx.fillStyle(star.col, a);
+      gfx.fillPoints(this._rotatedStarPts(cx, cy, star.s, rot), true);
+      gfx.fillStyle(0xffffff, wA);
+      gfx.fillCircle(cx, cy, star.s * 0.32);
+
+      remaining.push(star);
+    }
+    this.rainStars = remaining;
+  }
 
-      scene.tweens.add({

-        y:        gfx.y + Phaser.Math.Between(450, 600),

-        alpha:    0,

-        onComplete: () => { gfx.destroy(); },
-      });
+  /** 8각 별 좌표를 회전·스케일·이동 적용해 정적 버퍼에 in-place 기록 (GC 제로) */
+  private _rotatedStarPts(cx: number, cy: number, s: number, rot: number): { x: number; y: number }[] {
+    const buf  = LegacyAbility._STAR_BUF;
+    const norm = LegacyAbility._STAR_NORM;
+    const cos  = Math.cos(rot);
+    const sin  = Math.sin(rot);
+    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
+      const p  = norm[i]!;
+      buf[i]!.x = cx + (p.x * cos - p.y * sin) * s;
+      buf[i]!.y = cy + (p.x * sin + p.y * cos) * s;
     }
+    return buf;
   }
 
   // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

+    this.rainStars = [];
   }
```

```
1	import { BaseAbility } from './BaseAbility';
2	import type { GameSceneAPI } from './types';
3	import { ARCHIEVE_PARAMS } from '../config/abilityParams';
4	
5	/**
6	 * 아카이브 (SR) — 점수 배율 / 점수마다 보너스
7	 * 수치: ARCHIEVE_PARAMS 참조
8	 */
9	export class ArchieveAbility extends BaseAbility {
10	  private lastArchieveScore = 0;
11	  private isProcessingBonus = false;
12	  private accum = 0;
13	
14	  override getTickScore(base: number): number {
15	    this.accum += base * ARCHIEVE_PARAMS.scoreMultiplierExtra;
16	    const bonus = Math.floor(this.accum);
17	    this.accum -= bonus;
18	    return base + bonus;
19	  }
20	
21	  override onScoreMilestone(score: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
22	    if (this.isProcessingBonus) return;
23	    if (score % ARCHIEVE_PARAMS.bonusInterval === 0 && score > this.lastArchieveScore) {
24	      this.lastArchieveScore = score;
25	      this.isProcessingBonus = true;
26	      api.addAbilityBonus(ARCHIEVE_PARAMS.bonusScore);
27	      this.isProcessingBonus = false;
28	    }
29	  }
30	}
31
```

```
1	import Phaser from 'phaser';
2	import { BaseAbility } from './BaseAbility';
3	import type { GameSceneAPI } from './types';
4	import type PoolablePoopBase from '../objects/PoolablePoopBase';
5	import { MAEHWA_PARAMS } from '../config/abilityParams';
6	
7	/**
8	 * 매화 (SR) — 이동속도 버프 / 점수마다 칼 베기
9	 * 수치: MAEHWA_PARAMS 참조
10	 */
11	export class MaehwaAbility extends BaseAbility {
12	  private lastMaehwaScore = 0;
13	
14	  override getPlayerSpeedBonus(): number {
15	    return MAEHWA_PARAMS.speedBonus;
16	  }
17	
18	  // ★2+: 특수 똥 수집 시 추가 점수 (MAEHWA_PARAMS.awake2SpecialBonus)
19	  override onCollectSpecial(_type: import('./types').SpecialPoopType): number {
20	    return this.awakeningLevel >= 2 ? MAEHWA_PARAMS.awake2SpecialBonus : 0;
21	  }
22	
23	  override onScoreMilestone(score: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
24	    if (score % MAEHWA_PARAMS.slashInterval === 0 && score > this.lastMaehwaScore) {
25	      this.lastMaehwaScore = score;
26	      this.slashClosestPoops(MAEHWA_PARAMS.slashCount, api);
27	    }
28	  }
29	
30	  private slashClosestPoops(count: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
31	    const active = api.poops
32	      .getChildren()
33	      .filter(p => {
34	        const sp = p as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite;
35	        return sp.active && sp.y < api.player.y;
36	      }) as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite[];
37	
38	    active.sort((a, b) => {
39	      const da = Phaser.Math.Distance.Between(api.player.x, api.player.y, a.x, a.y);
40	      const db = Phaser.Math.Distance.Between(api.player.x, api.player.y, b.x, b.y);
41	      return da - db;
42	    });
43	
44	    active.slice(0, count).forEach(poop => {
45	      this.drawSlashEffect(poop.x, poop.y, api);
46	      this.spawnPetals(poop.x, poop.y, api);
47	      (poop as PoolablePoopBase).recycle();
48	    });
49	  }
50	
51	  /** 가운데가 넓은 대각선(↗) 칼날 이펙트 */
52	  private drawSlashEffect(cx: number, cy: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
53	    const gfx = api.scene.add.graphics().setDepth(120);
54	
55	    // 칼날: fillPoints로 ↗ 방향 마름모꼴 (끝은 뾰족, 중앙은 넓음)
56	    const len = 26; // 반길이
57	    const wid = 7;  // 중앙 최대 반폭
58	    // ↗ 방향 slash: (cx-len, cy+len) → (cx+len, cy-len) 축
59	    gfx.fillStyle(0xff3366, 0.9);
60	    gfx.fillPoints([
61	      new Phaser.Geom.Point(cx - len, cy + len),          // 좌하 끝
62	      new Phaser.Geom.Point(cx - wid * 0.4, cy + wid),    // 좌상 측면
63	      new Phaser.Geom.Point(cx + len, cy - len),          // 우상 끝
64	      new Phaser.Geom.Point(cx + wid * 0.4, cy - wid),    // 우하 측면
65	    ], true);
66	
67	    // 외곽 흰색 글로우
68	    gfx.lineStyle(3, 0xffffff, 0.5);
69	    gfx.lineBetween(cx - len, cy + len, cx + len, cy - len);
70	
71	    api.scene.tweens.add({
72	      targets: gfx,
73	      alpha: 0,
74	      duration: 300,
```

```
  slashInterval: 100,       // 칼 베기 점수 간격
  slashCount: 3,            // 한 번에 베는 똥 개수
  awake2SpecialBonus: 5,    // ★2+ 특수 똥 수집 추가 점수
  synergyBurstInterval: 2000,
  synergyBurstBonus: 150,    // 매화×매화 버스트 발동 시 고정 보너스 점수
} as const;

export const MAEHWA_DESC = {
  basicEffect: `이동 속도 +${MAEHWA_PARAMS.speedBonus}px/s`,
  specialAbility: `${MAEHWA_PARAMS.slashInterval}점마다 위쪽 똥 ${MAEHWA_PARAMS.slashCount}개 칼로 제거`,
} as const;

  bonusScore: 20,             // 보너스 점수
```

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**분석 결과:**
- `ArchieveAbility` / `RGradeAbility` — 그래픽 오브젝트 없음, 완전 무결 ✓
- `MaehwaAbility.spawnPetals` — 100점마다 Graphics 7개 × 3마리 = **21개 동시 생성** (각 400-700ms 생존). Graphics는 WebGL에서 개별 draw call → 21 draw call 버스트.
- `MaehwaAbility.drawSlashEffect` — 300ms 순간 플래시 1개, 무시 가능 ✓

`glow_dot` Image로 교체하면, 같은 텍스처를 가진 Image 21개를 Phaser WebGL 렌더러가 **자동 배치(batch)** 처리하여 21 draw call → 1 draw call이 됩니다. 이것이 `spawnOraFlame`을 Graphics에서 Image로 바꿨을 때와 같은 원리입니다.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/johyunduk/Projects/ddong-avoid-game/src/abilities/MaehwaAbility.ts",
  "old_string": "  /** 붉은 매화 잎 여러 개 낙화 */\n  private spawnPetals(cx: number, cy: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {\n    const PETAL_COUNT = 7;\n    for (let i = 0; i < PETAL_COUNT; i++) {\n      const petal = api.scene.add.graphics().setDepth(121);\n      // 초기 위치: 파괴된 똥 주변 랜덤\n      const startX = cx + Phaser.Math.Between(-12, 12);\n      const startY = cy + Phaser.Math.Between(-12, 12);\n      petal.setPosition(startX, startY);\n\n      // 작은 타원형 꽃잎\n      const petalW = Phaser.Math.Between(4, 7);\n      const petalH = Phaser.Math.Between(7, 11);\n      petal.fillStyle(0xff2255, 0.85 + Math.random() * 0.15);\n      petal.fillEllipse(0, 0, petalW, petalH);\n      petal.setAngle(Phaser.Math.Between(0, 360));\n\n      // 바람에 날리며 낙하\n      api.scene.tweens.add({\n        targets: petal,\n        x: startX + Phaser.Math.Between(-25, 25),\n        y: startY + Phaser.Math.Between(30, 60),\n        angle: petal.angle + Phaser.Math.Between(-120, 120),\n        alpha: 0,\n        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(400, 700),\n        ease: 'Sine.easeIn',\n        onComplete: () => petal.destroy(),\n      });\n    }\n  }",
  "new_string": "  /** 붉은 매화 잎 여러 개 낙화 — glow_dot Image 사용으로 WebGL 배치 처리 (21 draw call → 1) */\n  private spawnPetals(cx: number, cy: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {\n    const PETAL_COUNT = 7;\n    ensureGlowDot(api.scene);\n    for (let i = 0; i < PETAL_COUNT; i++) {\n      const startX = cx + Phaser.Math.Between(-12, 12);\n      const startY = cy + Phaser.Math.Between(-12, 12);\n      const scale  = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0.04, 0.08);\n\n      const img = api.scene.add.image(startX, startY, 'glow_dot')\n        .setDepth(121)\n        .setBlendMode(Phaser.BlendModes.ADD)\n        .setTint(0xff2255)\n        .setScale(scale)\n        .setAlpha(0.85 + Math.random() * 0.15);\n\n      api.scene.tweens.add({\n        targets: img,\n        x: startX + Phaser.Math.Between(-25, 25),\n        y: startY + Phaser.Math.Between(30, 60),\n        alpha: 0,\n        scale: 0,\n        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(400, 700),\n        ease: 'Sine.easeIn',\n        onComplete: () => { if (img.active) img.destroy(); },\n      });\n    }\n  }"
}
```
